TL;DR: 本文提出了一种流映射学习（FML）框架，用于直接从解数据中建模未知的非局部偏微分方程。该方法不学习或逼近底层的非局部算子，而是学习有限时间演化算子，在一维和二维分数阶扩散与波动方程的数值实验中展示了仅用短观测窗口即可实现准确稳定的长时间预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非局部偏微分方程在许多应用中广泛出现，但由于非局部算子的存在，这类方程的建模和学习往往非常困难。现有的方法通常需要显式处理或逼近非局部算子，这带来了额外的计算复杂性和建模难度。

Method: 提出了流映射学习（FML）框架，直接学习有限时间演化算子而非底层的非局部算子。针对谱表示和网格表示两种解表示方式，分别开发了两种互补的公式化方法（模态空间和节点空间）。

Result: 在一维和二维分数阶扩散方程及波动方程的数值实验中，该方法仅使用短的观测窗口即可实现准确且稳定的长时间预测，验证了所提出框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的FML框架为学习未知非局部动力学提供了一种有效的数据驱动方法，无需显式评估非局部算子即可完成建模和预测。

Abstract: Nonlocal partial differential equations arise in many applications but are often difficult to model and learn because of the presence of nonlocal operators. We present a flow-map learning (FML) framework for modeling unknown nonlocal PDEs directly from solution data. Rather than learning or approximating the underlying nonlocal operators, the proposed approach learns the finite-time evolution operator in either modal or nodal space. Two complementary formulations are developed for spectral and grid-based solution representations. Numerical experiments on one- and two-dimensional fractional diffusion and wave equations demonstrate accurate and stable long-time prediction using only short observation windows. The proposed approach provides an effective data-driven framework for learning unknown nonlocal dynamics without explicit evaluation of nonlocal operators.

</details>


### [56] [DSETA: A Dual-Stage Continual Learning Framework for Travel Time Prediction in Dynamic Traffic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00402)
*Yanming Lyu,Yue Cheng,Lingkun Li,Ruipeng Gao,Xinyue Liu,Hui Gao,Qiang Ni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DSETA，一个双阶段增量更新的ETA预测框架，通过将持续学习分为日内和日间两个阶段来分别捕捉短期波动与长期分布漂移，并引入历史交通知识巩固模块防止灾难性遗忘，已在滴滴生产环境部署，在三大城市A/B测试中MAE分别降低6.62%、0.73%和2.40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ETA预测方法无法适应大城市中日益动态的交通拥堵模式，要么难以应对不规则交通和突发拥堵，要么在面对新数据分布时未能解耦长期趋势与短期波动，导致真实场景下模型性能退化。

Method: 提出DSETA双阶段框架：(1)日内学习阶段，完全依赖实时数据适应假期、事故等短期交通模式；(2)日间学习阶段，利用短时间窗口的聚合历史数据捕捉季节性趋势和交通网络演化等长期分布漂移；(3)引入历史交通知识巩固模块防止灾难性遗忘，保留常规模式知识。

Result: 在滴滴真实数据集上的离线和在线实验中均验证了有效性。北京、武汉、西安三城市的在线A/B测试中MAE分别降低6.62%、0.73%和2.40%，且已部署至滴滴生产环境，每日处理数亿请求。

Conclusion: DSETA通过日内与日间的双阶段持续学习设计，有效兼顾了短期动态适应与长期分布漂移的捕捉，同时利用知识巩固机制避免遗忘，在工业级规模下展现出显著的性能提升和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Estimated Time of Arrival (ETA) prediction is a core component of intelligent transportation systems. As traffic congestion patterns become increasingly dynamic in large cities, maintaining high prediction accuracy poses a major challenge for ride-hailing platforms. Existing methods either fail to adapt to irregular traffic patterns and sudden congestion, or suffer from new distributions without disentangling long-term trends from short-term fluctuations, thereby degrading model performance in real-world scenarios. To address this challenge, we propose DSETA, an incrementally updated Dual-Stage ETA prediction framework. Specifically, the continual learning process is divided into \textit{inter-day} and \textit{intra-day} stages. We first design the \textit{intra-day} learning stage, which relies entirely on real-time data to enable dynamic adaptation to short-term traffic patterns caused by events like holidays or accidents. Next, we develop the \textit{inter-day} learning stage, which leverages aggregated historical data from a short time window to capture knowledge of long-term distribution shifts, such as seasonal trends and traffic network evolution. Subsequently, to prevent catastrophic forgetting and preserve knowledge of regular patterns, we explore a \textit{Historical Traffic Knowledge Consolidation} module. Finally, we validate DSETA's effectiveness and robustness through extensive offline and online experiments conducted on real-world datasets from DiDi's platform. Online A/B tests across three major cities including Beijing, Wuhan, and Xi'an consistently demonstrated performance gains, achieving MAE reductions of 6.62\%, 0.73\%, and 2.40\% respectively. This framework has been successfully deployed in DiDi's production environment, processing hundreds of millions of daily requests and validating its strong performance in industrial applications.

</details>


### [57] [Unleashing the Potential of Large Language Models: A Blueprint for Real-Time, Enterprise-Ready Deployments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00419)
*Muhammad Faizan Raza,Shuo,Yang,Satish Mahadevan Srinivasan,Joanna F. DeFranco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的、基于软件设计模式的LLMOps架构，整合实时数据摄取、持续学习、RAG和人机反馈循环，用于解决大语言模型在实时监管场景中的知识过时、灾难性遗忘、幻觉和反馈循环薄弱等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在实时、受监管的环境中部署面临知识过时、灾难性遗忘、幻觉和薄弱反馈循环等核心挑战，现有方案缺乏统一且可审计的端到端运营架构。

Method: 提出四个基于既有软件设计模式的贡献：1) AIPO自适应摄取模式编排器（FreshStreamBench评测）；2) STAR+FAR持续学习方法，使用稀疏时间适配器路由和新鲜度感知回放；3) SAGE，一种SLO感知的自适应检索策略，预测每条查询的段落预算以满足尾延迟目标；4) 自动化反馈驱动的收敛阶段，带RLHF触发机制。

Result: 该架构在延迟-成本-准确率之间实现了更好的平衡，同时支持高风控行业（如医疗和金融）所需的可审计性和回滚能力。

Conclusion: 该统一LLMOps架构通过模式驱动的模块化设计，为实时受监管环境中的LLM部署提供了兼顾性能、可审计性和可回滚性的系统性解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models deployed in real-time, regulated settings face knowledge staleness, catastrophic forgetting, hallucination, and weak feedback loops. We present a unified, pattern-driven LLMOps architecture integrating real-time data ingestion, continual learning, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), and human-in-the-loop feedback into a single operational pipeline. Four contributions map to established software design patterns: an adaptive ingestion pattern orchestrator (AIPO) evaluated with FreshStreamBench; STAR+FAR continual learning with sparse temporal adapter routing and freshness-aware replay; SAGE, an SLO-aware adaptive retrieval policy predicting a per-query passage budget to meet tail-latency targets; and an automated feedback-driven convergence stage with RLHF triggers. The result reduces latency-cost-accuracy trade-offs while supporting auditability and rollback for high-risk sectors such as health care and finance.

</details>


### [58] [HP-JEPA: Hierarchical Partitioning for Multi-Resolution Graph Joint-Embedding Predictive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00491)
*Ruichen Xu,Jingxiang Qu,Wenhan Gao,Jiaxing Zhang,Linsey Pang,Ravid Shwartz-Ziv,Yann LeCun,Yuefan Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HP-JEPA，一种用于图自监督学习的层级多分辨率联合嵌入预测框架。它通过构建从粗到细的多级图分区，并在每个分辨率上独立执行上下文-目标潜在空间预测，最后通过拼接或任务特定加权整合不同尺度的图表示。在8个图基准任务上，HP-JEPA在6个任务上优于固定分辨率的Graph-JEPA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）依赖单一预定义的图分区，导致学习到的表示偏向某一结构粒度，无法捕捉图在不同尺度上的互补模式。为了克服这一局限，需要一种能够同时利用局部、区域和全局结构信息的方法。

Method: HP-JEPA将每个图组织成有序的从粗到细的分区分辨率库，使用在线编码器、指数移动平均目标编码器和潜在预测器在每个分辨率上分别进行上下文-目标潜在预测，随后通过拼接或任务特定分辨率加权整合各分辨率的图表示。

Result: 在7个图分类基准和1个图回归基准上，HP-JEPA在8个任务中的6个上优于固定分辨率的Graph-JEPA基线。按图大小分层分析显示，在三个代表数据集的多数图大小四分位数上，HP-JEPA的准确率也高于Graph-JEPA。

Conclusion: 层级多分辨率分区能有效提升可迁移图表示学习的性能，证明了多尺度结构信息对图自监督学习的重要性。

Abstract: Graph self-supervised learning aims to learn transferable representations from large-scale unlabeled graph data. Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) avoid explicit negative-pair construction and raw-input reconstruction by predicting masked targets directly in latent space. However, existing graph JEPAs typically rely on a single predefined graph partition, biasing the learned representations toward one structural granularity and limiting their ability to capture complementary patterns at different graph scales. To address this limitation, we propose HP-JEPA, a hierarchical partitioning framework for multi-resolution graph joint-embedding prediction. HP-JEPA organizes each graph into an ordered bank of coarse-to-fine partition resolutions and performs context-target latent prediction separately at each resolution using an online encoder, an exponential-moving-average target encoder, and a latent predictor. The resulting resolution-specific graph representations are subsequently integrated through concatenation or task-specific resolution weighting, allowing downstream models to combine complementary local, regional, and global structural information. Experiments on seven graph classification benchmarks and one graph regression benchmark show that HP-JEPA outperforms the fixed-resolution Graph-JEPA baseline on 6 of 8 tasks, improving upon Graph-JEPA on most evaluated benchmarks. Size-stratified analyses further show that HP-JEPA achieves higher accuracy than Graph-JEPA in most evaluated graph-size quartiles on three representative datasets. These results highlight the effectiveness of hierarchical multi-resolution partitioning for transferable graph representation learning.

</details>


### [59] [Agentic Graph Token Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00542)
*Zhuoyi Peng,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出智能体图令牌推理（agentic graph token reasoning），将图编码器的图令牌生成过程嵌入到大语言模型的逐步推理中，模型每一步自主选择图视图和粒度，按需调用图编码器生成令牌并拼接进上下文，通过三阶段训练管道（自监督阅读、令牌鲁棒轨迹、偏好优化）实现，在七个图领域大幅超越基线并零样本泛化到未见领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图令牌的LLM图分析方法采用静态、单次的方式：在模型看到目标任务之前就编码一个预定义的图视图，且从不修改，未充分利用模型的逐步推理能力。

Method: 将图令牌化重新构思为推理过程本身的一部分：每步由模型选择图视图和粒度，按需调用图编码器生成对应图令牌，拼接进运行上下文；采用三阶段训练管道：(i) 自监督任务教模型读取异构图令牌，(ii) 带图令牌一致性正则化的令牌鲁棒轨迹阶段，(iii) 偏好优化奖励图令牌证据与节点文本证据一致的轨迹。

Result: 在横跨七个图领域的评估中，模型大幅超越广泛的基线方法，且无需任何针对目标任务的微调即可零样本迁移到未见领域。

Conclusion: 本文推动基于LLM的图分析从静态图令牌编码器走向图原生的智能体范式，实现了图令牌空间中的逐步推理与轨迹相关的动态令牌读取。

Abstract: Graphs model relational data throughout science and industry, from citation networks to product co-purchase graphs. Because the nodes of many such graphs carry rich text, a growing line of work applies large language models (LLMs) to graph analysis. The most graph-native of these methods use graph tokens: a graph encoder compresses a graph view, such as a node, its k-hop neighbourhood, or a cluster, into a short block of continuous tokens that jointly encodes node attributes and topology and is read directly by the model. Existing methods, however, use graph tokens in a static single-shot manner: they encode one predefined graph view before the model has even seen the target and never revise it, leaving the model's step-by-step reasoning ability unused. We introduce agentic graph token reasoning, which recasts graph tokenization as part of the reasoning process itself. At each step, the model chooses which graph view to encode and at what granularity; a graph encoder is invoked on demand to materialise the corresponding graph tokens; and the resulting block is spliced into the running context. The model thus reasons step by step in the graph token space, and the tokens it reads are trajectory-dependent. We realise this with a three-stage training pipeline: (i) self-supervised tasks that teach the model to read heterogeneous graph tokens, (ii) a token-robust trajectory stage with a graph-token consistency regulariser, and (iii) preference optimisation that rewards trajectories in which the graph-token evidence and the node-text evidence agree. Across evaluations spanning seven graph domains, our models outperform a broad set of baselines by a large margin and transfer zero-shot to unseen domains without any per-target fine-tuning. More broadly, this work pushes LLM-based graph analysis from static graph-token encoders towards a graph-native agent paradigm.

</details>


### [60] [Crushing the Evidence: A Dual-Penalty Evasion Framework for Fooling White-Box Explainable AI Auditors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00566)
*Niraj Kumar,Harsh Kasyap*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种白盒、梯度正则化的对抗性解释逃避攻击框架，通过连续嵌入双重惩罚机制，在训练阶段直接惩罚触发特征的梯度，将逃避逻辑原生嵌入模型参数，无需依赖OOD脚手架包装器，从而生成无异常足迹的平滑分布内预测，成功绕过条件异常检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对抗性解释攻击依赖于OOD检测器脚手架，容易被基于异常扰动足迹的防御机制识别和中和。作者旨在揭示一种更隐蔽、更强大的攻击方式，不留下可被异常检测捕获的痕迹，从而暴露解释性管道中尚未被充分认识的关键漏洞。

Method: 提出白盒、梯度正则化的逃避攻击框架，采用连续嵌入双重惩罚框架，在分布内数据的训练过程中直接对触发特征的梯度施加惩罚，将逃避逻辑原生化地嵌入模型参数中，避免使用OOD脚手架包装器，从而产生平滑的、分布内的预测，不留下异常足迹。

Result: 在四个基准表格数据集（COMPAS、German Credit、IEEE-CIS、Communities & Crime）上的实验表明，该方法能够将目标特征归因压至接近零（<0.02），维持超过90%的攻击成功率，并从根本上绕过条件异常检测。

Conclusion: 现有的基于异常检测的防御机制对这类白盒梯度正则化攻击是无效的，解释性管道的安全性需要重新审视，特别是针对将逃避逻辑嵌入模型参数内部的新型攻击形态。

Abstract: Post-hoc model explainers such as LIME, SHAP, and Integrated Gradients are widely deployed to audit models in high-stakes sensitive domains, including finance, healthcare, and social welfare. This ensures the model's transparency and acceptability. However, a few studies have examined potential attacks in the explainability pipeline. Adversaries can attempt to conceal algorithmic biases or backdoors using adversarial explanation attacks. These attacks have relied on scaffolding out-of-distribution (OOD) detectors that toggle predictions when queried by an explainer. Consequently, defenses have been developed to successfully neutralize these black-box attacks by identifying their anomalous perturbation footprints. In this paper, we demonstrate a critical vulnerability by introducing a more potent white-box, gradient-regularized evasion attack framework. By employing a continuous-embedding dual-penalty framework, we directly penalize trigger feature gradients during training on in-distribution data. Since our approach embeds the evasion logic natively into the model parameters, without relying on OOD scaffolding wrappers, it generates smooth, in-distribution predictions that leave no anomaly footprint. Empirical evaluations across four benchmark tabular datasets (COMPAS, German Credit, IEEE-CIS, and Communities & Crime) confirm that our method systematically crushes target feature attribution to near-zero (<0.02), maintains >90% Attack Success Rates, and fundamentally bypasses Conditional Anomaly Detection.

</details>


### [61] [Fairness Auditing: Lower Bounds on Company Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00568)
*Rachit Verma,Padala Manisha,Sujit Gujar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在有限审计资源下，公平性审计后模型仍可进行规避操纵的不可避免程度。通过将公平性审计建模为公司与预算受限审计者之间的最小-最大优化问题，推导了两种审计体制下最坏情况的人口统计均等偏差的下界，并通过实验验证了理论极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已证明表达力足够的模型可以规避任何黑盒公平性审计策略，但尚未量化在有限审计资源下这种规避操纵的程度有多大，本文旨在补充这一缺口。

Method: 将公平性审计形式化为计算不受限的公司与预算受限审计者之间的最小-最大博弈。考虑两种审计体制：(i) 使用固定规模审计集进行认证的预算受限审计者；(ii) 额外要求审计集在α逼近范围内估计公平性的预算受限α容忍审计者。对两种设置推导最坏情况下审计后人口统计均等偏差的显式下界。

Result: 推导出两种审计体制下最坏情况审计后人口统计均等偏差的下界，是审计预算、群体不平衡和公平容忍度的函数。实验使用简单审计集构建启发式方法（线性与神经网络分类器）验证了这些理论极限。

Conclusion: 增加审计资源可以降低但不能消除审计后操纵的空间，这凸显了有限预算公平性认证的根本局限性。

Abstract: Fairness audits are increasingly mandated in high-stakes applications such as hiring, lending, and automated decision-making. Recent work has established fundamental impossibility results for black-box fairness auditing, showing that sufficiently expressive models can evade any auditing strategy. We complement these results by quantifying the extent of unavoidable post-audit manipulation under finite audit resources. We formulate fairness auditing as a min-max optimization between a computationally unbounded company and a budget-constrained auditor. We study two auditing regimes: (i) a budgeted auditor that certifies fairness using a fixed-size audit set, and (ii) a budgeted α-tolerant auditor that additionally requires the audit set to estimate the fairness of the certified model within an α approximation. For both settings, we derive explicit lower bounds on the worst-case post-audit demographic parity deviation as functions of the audit budget, group imbalance, and fairness tolerance. Finally, we empirically illustrate these theoretical limits using simple audit-set construction heuristics with linear and neural network classifiers. Our results demonstrate that increasing audit resources reduces, but does not eliminate, the scope for post-audit manipulation, highlighting fundamental limitations of finite-budget fairness certification.

</details>


### [62] [CoSynFlow: Conformal Symplectic Neural Flows for Cross-System Prediction of Dissipative Hamiltonian Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00571)
*Baige Xu,Takaharu Yaguchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoSynFlow，一种保形辛神经流方法，用于学习耗散哈密顿动力学的连续时间解算子。该方法通过组合辛剪切映射与显式共形缩放，在构造上保持共形辛结构，并可在单一模型中预测未见系统的解映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子方法大多仅优化预测精度，未显式施加动力学的几何结构约束。结构保持模型（如SympNets、Symplectic Neural Flows）虽有处理保守哈密顿系统，但无法应对具有共形辛结构的耗散哈密顿系统，其中辛形式按耗散决定的共形因子演化。

Method: CoSynFlow将辛剪切映射与显式共形缩放组合，通过构造保证共形辛结构的保持；同时以有限维哈密顿描述符和耗散参数为条件，使单个训练模型无需重训练即可预测未见系统的解映射，并支持物理信息训练。

Result: CoSynFlow将结构误差维持在机器精度水平，在长时程预测中取得最低误差，且支持物理信息训练。

Conclusion: CoSynFlow有效弥补了现有结构保持神经算子方法在处理耗散哈密顿系统上的空白，兼具高结构保持精度与良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Learning solution operators for differential equations is a central problem in scientific machine learning. However, many neural operator methods optimize prediction accuracy without explicitly enforcing the geometric structure of the dynamics. Structure-preserving models such as SympNets and Symplectic Neural Flows address this issue for conservative Hamiltonian systems by preserving the symplectic form. In dissipative Hamiltonian systems with conformal symplectic structure, however, the symplectic form evolves according to a conformal factor determined by the dissipation. We propose CoSynFlow, a conformal symplectic neural flow for learning continuous-time solution maps of dissipative Hamiltonian dynamics. CoSynFlow composes symplectic shear maps with explicit conformal scaling, preserving the conformal symplectic structure by construction. By conditioning it on a finite-dimensional Hamiltonian descriptor and the dissipation parameter, a single trained model predicts solution maps for unseen systems without retraining. CoSynFlow keeps the structure error at machine precision, attains the lowest long-horizon error, and admits physics-informed training.

</details>


### [63] [From field-scale to large-scale spectral libraries: Tabular foundation models in soil spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00608)
*Viacheslav Barkov,Jonas Schmidinger,Robin Gebbers,Martin Atzmueller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了多种回归模型和降维方法在土壤光谱建模中的表现，涵盖85个回归任务。TabPFN（表格基础模型）在完整光谱上表现最佳，结合PLS潜在变量后进一步提升了预测精度，超越了PLSR、随机森林等传统基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可见-近红外和中红外光谱可以快速、低成本地预测土壤属性，但高维且高度共线的光谱数据在进行机器学习建模时仍具有挑战性。作者旨在系统性地比较不同回归模型和降维策略，为光谱校准模型的选择提供基于证据的指导。

Method: 在85个回归任务（涵盖田间尺度数字土壤制图和全球土壤光谱库）上，系统比较了TabPFN（上下文学习的表格基础模型）、卷积神经网络（CNN）、Cubist规则回归、随机森林和偏最小二乘回归（PLSR）。使用完整光谱以及经PCA和PLS潜在变量降维后的特征进行建模。

Result: TabPFN在各类尺度任务中均表现最佳，包括含数万土壤样本的大型光谱库任务。TabPFN直接应用于完整光谱即超越所有经典基线，说明显式降维并非获得强性能的必要条件。结合PLS潜在变量与TabPFN取得了整体最优预测结果。

Conclusion: PLS作为有效的降维策略可提升所有模型的性能。PLSR的长期优势与现代表格基础模型（TabPFN）具有互补性，研究为不同操作尺度下光谱校准模型的选择提供了循证指导。

Abstract: Visible and near-infrared (vis-NIR) and mid-infrared (MIR) spectroscopy enable rapid, cost-effective prediction of soil properties. Yet, translating high-dimensional, highly collinear spectra into accurate soil property predictions remains challenging, particularly when employing machine learning. We systematically investigated regression models and dimensionality reduction approaches for spectroscopic modeling across 85 regression tasks from open benchmark datasets in pedometrics spanning field-scale digital soil mapping and a global soil spectral library. We compared an in-context learning tabular foundation model (TabPFN), a convolutional neural network (CNN), rule-based regression (Cubist), Random Forest, and partial least squares regression (PLSR) using full spectra as well as features derived from principal component analysis (PCA) and partial least squares (PLS) latent variables. TabPFN consistently delivered the best overall performance across scales, including large spectral library tasks with tens of thousands of soil samples. Notably, TabPFN applied directly to full spectra already surpassed all classical baselines, showing that explicit dimensionality reduction is not strictly required for strong performance. Further improvements were achieved through PLS, which proved to be an effective dimensionality reduction strategy for all models. Combining PLS latent variables with TabPFN yielded the best predictions overall. Our findings provide evidence-based guidance for spectroscopic calibration model selection across operational scales, demonstrating that the long-standing advantages of PLSR and modern tabular foundation models complement each other in chemometrics.

</details>


### [64] [RHEA: Reliability-Harmonized Reconstruction and Assignment for Robust Multimodal-Attributed Graph Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00621)
*Yinlin Zhu,Di Wu,Ziyu Han,Zekai Chenm,Wang Luo,Miao Hu,Guocong Quan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RHEA框架，用于多模态属性图（MAG）聚类，能够从图邻域共识中估计节点特定的模态可靠性，在属性噪声或缺失条件下显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAG聚类方法假设所有节点的模态可靠性相同，在属性干净完整时能有效融合互补模态，但在属性有噪声或缺失时性能大幅下降。实际中模态可靠性是节点特定的，例如图像可能损坏或缺失，文本描述可能不完整或有噪声。

Method: 利用属性同质性假设，从图邻域共识中估计节点特定的模态可靠性。RHEA框架包含：（1）从图邻域重建不可靠或缺失的模态；（2）在可靠性感知融合中自适应加权模态；（3）执行拓扑感知的最优传输聚类，包含可靠性感知的传输分配和邻域共识分配蒸馏；（4）将重建表示的置信度纳入聚类目标，使不确定的重建按比例贡献。

Result: 在四个MAG基准测试和五种属性条件下，RHEA一致优于最强基线，并且随着属性质量恶化，NMI增益持续增大。

Conclusion: RHEA通过节点特定的模态可靠性估计和传播，有效解决了噪声或缺失属性下的MAG聚类问题，且属性质量越差其优势越明显。

Abstract: Multimodal-attributed graphs (MAGs), whose nodes carry heterogeneous attributes such as text and images over a relational structure, have become a fundamental substrate for label-free entity grouping tasks, including community discovery and product segmentation. Existing MAG clustering methods effectively integrate complementary modalities when attributes are clean and complete, but degrade substantially under noisy or missing attributes because they implicitly assume equal modality reliability across all nodes. In practice, modality reliability is inherently node-specific: images may be corrupted or absent, while textual descriptions are incomplete or noisy. We argue that, under attribute homophily, graph neighborhoods naturally provide supervision-free evidence for estimating node-specific modality reliability. Based on this insight, we propose RHEA, a reliability-aware framework for MAG clustering that estimates node-specific modality reliability from neighborhood consensus and propagates this signal throughout the clustering pipeline. RHEA reconstructs unreliable or missing modalities from graph neighborhoods, adaptively weights modalities during reliability-aware fusion, and performs topology-aware optimal transport clustering with reliability-aware transport assignment and neighbor-consensus assignment distillation. Furthermore, the confidence of reconstructed representations is incorporated into the clustering objective, allowing uncertain reconstructions to contribute proportionally during optimization. Experiments on four MAG benchmarks under five attribute conditions show that RHEA consistently outperforms the strongest baseline, with NMI gains increasing as attribute quality deteriorates.

</details>


### [65] [Towards Effective Federated Multimodal Graph Learning via Navigating Multifaceted Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00623)
*Yinlin Zhu,Di Wu,Yi Zhang,Xunkai Li,Wang Luo,Wei-Jin Huang,Miao Hu,Guocong Quan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedTCR，这是首个针对联邦多模态图学习（FMGL）的系统性算法，通过两阶段训练范式处理任务异质性，并通过拓扑感知的跨模态路由机制联合解决模态和拓扑异质性，实验在7个领域上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的联邦图学习（FGL）方法直接应用于联邦多模态图学习（FMGL）时存在不足，因为它们无法应对去中心化多模态图中固有的多重异质性，包括任务异质性（不同客户端目标多样）、模态异质性（模态质量和语义领域不一致）以及拓扑异质性（拓扑模式差异大且跨模态相关性低）。

Method: FedTCR采用两阶段范式：联邦任务无关预训练+隔离的任务导向微调来处理任务异质性；引入拓扑感知的跨模态路由机制，每个客户端通过图结构信息指导的拓扑感知重要性加权聚合将模态特定知识蒸馏为紧凑原型，服务器评估跨客户端跨模态关系并路由有信息量的原型作为对比参考，驱动三层次跨模态对比学习以联合对齐跨客户端模态并保持判别性。

Result: 在7个不同领域上的实验表明，FedTCR在图中心任务和模态中心任务上均优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: FedTCR是首个系统性解决FMGL挑战的算法，通过拓扑感知的跨模态路由机制有效应对了去中心化多模态图中的多重异质性，为联邦多模态图学习提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal-attributed graphs (MAGs), where nodes carry heterogeneous semantic content across multiple modalities while edges encode relational dependencies, have been widely adopted across diverse domains. Federated multimodal graph learning (FMGL) extends federated graph learning (FGL) to MAGs, enabling collaborative optimization across decentralized MAGs without exposing raw data. However, naively applying existing FGL methods to FMGL is insufficient, as they fail to navigate the multifaceted heterogeneity inherent in decentralized MAGs, including task heterogeneity across diverse client objectives, modality heterogeneity from discrepant modality quality and semantic domains, and topology heterogeneity arising from divergent topological patterns with low cross-modality correlation. To address these challenges, we propose Federated multimodal graph learning with Topology-aware Cross-modal Routing (FedTCR), the first systematic algorithm designed for FMGL. To handle task heterogeneity, FedTCR employs a two-stage paradigm that comprises federated task-agnostic pre-training followed by isolated task-oriented fine-tuning. To jointly address modality and topology heterogeneity, FedTCR introduces a topology-aware cross-modal routing mechanism. Concretely, each client distills modality-specific knowledge into compact prototypes via topology-aware importance-weighted aggregation informed by graph structure; the server then evaluates cross-client cross-modal relationships among these structure-informed prototypes and routes informative ones as contrastive references, driving a tri-level cross-modal contrastive learning scheme that jointly aligns cross-client modalities while preserving discrimination. Experiments across 7 domains demonstrate that FedTCR outperforms state-of-the-art baselines on both graph-centric and modality-centric tasks.

</details>


### [66] [Relative Parameter Importance in Task-Agnostic Replay-Free Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00630)
*Malavika Suresh,Ikechukwu Nkisi-Orji,Nirmalie Wiratunga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的持续学习方法，通过相对参数重要性度量来平衡稳定性和可塑性，在无法访问旧任务数据和任务ID的约束下，实现有效的知识迁移，并在文本分类和生成任务上验证了方法的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习需要在保持旧任务性能（稳定性）和学习新任务（可塑性）之间取得平衡，同时实现知识迁移。现有方法在无旧数据和无任务ID的条件下仍存在局限性，特别是在参数更新策略上缺乏灵活性，限制了向后知识迁移的能力。

Method: 提出相对参数重要性（relative parameter-importance）度量，该指标同时考量每个参数对当前任务和过去任务的重要性。高相对重要性的参数将受到强正则化以维护旧任务稳定性，低相对重要性的参数则可自由更新。与现有方法不同，该算法允许在参数对过去任务重要性高但相对重要性低时更新这些参数，从而实现向后知识迁移。

Result: 在类增量学习和域增量学习的文本分类问题上，相比现有最先进的持续学习方法取得了性能提升，并提供了向文本生成任务扩展的见解。

Conclusion: 相对参数重要性度量能够更好地解决持续学习中的稳定性-可塑性权衡问题，同时支持向后知识迁移，为无任务ID、无旧数据场景下的持续学习提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Achieving continual learning (CL) with deep neural networks requires balancing stability and plasticity while enabling knowledge transfer. In this work, we focus on offline learning algorithms under the constraints: (I) no access to training data from prior tasks (II) no access to task-id at inference time. We introduce a novel measure, the relative parameter-importance, which measures the relative importance of each parameter with respect to both the current and past tasks. Parameters with high relative importance are interpreted as more important for maintaining past-task stability and thus heavily regularised, whereas parameters with low relative-importance are allowed to be more freely updated. Unlike existing methods, our approach allows the update of parameters with high past-task importance when they have low relative-importance, thus enabling backward knowledge transfer in addition to tackling the stability-plasticity trade-off. We demonstrate improvements against state-of-the-art CL methods on both class-incremental and domain-incremental learning text classification problems and provide insights for extending our method to text generation problems. Code available at: https://github.com/itsmemala/LACL

</details>


### [67] [Learning the Pareto Frontier of Predictive Models under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00632)
*Yiming Dong,Jiwei Zhao,Yang Young Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Frontier Learning框架，通过拼接白盒模型的内部表示和黑盒模型的预测输出，构建统一的目标域特征表示来训练轻量级监督学习器，在分布偏移场景下实现不劣于任何单一模型复用策略的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习流水线大量依赖预训练模型和基础模型，但这些模型在性能和使用方式上（白盒/黑盒）存在差异。在分布偏移场景下，没有任何单一策略（零样本应用、微调或直接训练）是普遍最优的，需要一种能综合多种模型优势的框架。

Method: 提出Frontier Learning框架：将多个候选模型（涵盖不同训练历史和访问权限）视为互补的信息源而非互斥选项。通过拼接白盒候选模型的内部表示和黑盒候选模型的预测输出，构造统一的目标域特征，然后在该特征上拟合带正则化的轻量级监督学习器。该假设类包含零样本复用、微调和直接训练作为特例，保证在训练样本上的经验风险最小化不劣于任何单一基线。

Result: 在模拟实验（涵盖不同源-目标兼容性程度）和两个真实分布偏移场景（DomainNet/VisDA视觉域适应和MIMIC-IV-Notes ICU临床死亡预测）中，Frontier Learning在所有设置下均达到或超越最强的个体复用策略，且在单一基线不可靠的偏移范围内增益最大。

Conclusion: Frontier Learning是一种有效的框架，能综合利用不同访问权限和训练历史的候选模型处理分布偏移问题，其理论保证和实验验证表明该方法在实际应用中具有稳健性和优越性。

Abstract: Modern machine learning pipelines increasingly rely on reusing pretrained and foundation models across downstream tasks. These pretrained models can differ not only in performance but also in how they can be used: some only provide black-box predictions, while others may permit white-box access to internal representations that can be probed or fine-tuned. When deployed to the target domain in the presence of distribution shift, no single strategy, including zero-shot application, fine-tuning, or directly training a target-specific model, is uniformly the best.
  In this work, we propose Frontier Learning, a framework that treats a library of candidate models spanning different training histories and access regimes as complementary sources of information rather than mutually exclusive alternatives. Frontier Learning constructs a unified target-domain feature by concatenating internal representations from white-box candidates as well as prediction outputs from black-box candidates, then fits a lightweight, regularized supervised learner on this concatenated representation using labeled target data. Because the resulting hypothesis class contains predictors obtained by zero-shot reuse, fine-tuning, and direct training as special cases, empirical risk minimization over the frontier learner is guaranteed to be no worse, on the training sample, than any individual baseline.
  We evaluate the framework in simulations spanning varying degrees of source-target compatibility and in two real-world distribution-shift settings: visual domain adaptation on DomainNet/VisDA and clinical mortality prediction across intensive care unit domains using MIMIC-IV-Notes. Across all settings, Frontier Learning matches or outperforms the strongest individual reuse strategy, with the largest gains arising precisely when no single baseline is reliable across the range of shift considered.

</details>


### [68] [Mitigating Backdoors via Decoy Shortcuts and Knowledge Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00732)
*Zixuan Zhu,Rui Wang,Lihua Jing,Jinwen Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为TR（Trapping and Removing）的训练时后门防御方法，通过引入轻量级"蜜罐"捷径分支来捕获后门知识，训练后直接丢弃该分支即可移除后门，无需额外数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后门攻击对依赖第三方数据训练的深度神经网络构成严重威胁，现有防御方法通常需要额外数据或复杂的后处理步骤，且难以在保持良性性能的同时有效隔离后门行为。

Method: 方法包括三个核心组件：(1) 引入轻量级捷径分支作为"蜜罐"来吸引和捕获后门知识；(2) 设计基于熵权重分配的知识解耦策略，促使中毒样本流向蜜罐分支，引导主网络专注于良性学习；(3) 提出自动捷径生成策略以提升对不同模型架构的泛化能力。

Result: 在四个基准数据集和五种模型架构上的大量实验表明，该方法能有效缓解多种后门攻击，同时保持良性数据上的性能。

Conclusion: 通过简单的"捕获并丢弃"策略，TR方法实现了无需额外数据的高效后门防御，为训练时防御提供了一种轻量且有效的新思路。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks pose a serious threat to deep neural networks, especially when training relies on third-party data, allowing adversaries to inject malicious behaviors through data poisoning. In this work, we reveal that backdoor behaviors tend to be absorbed by a simpler parallel branch when jointly trained with the main network. Motivated by this insight, we propose Trapping and Removing (TR), a simple yet effective training-time defense that introduces a lightweight shortcut branch as a "honeypot" to trap backdoor knowledge. After training, backdoors can be removed by discarding the shortcut, without requiring any additional data. To further enhance backdoor isolation while maintaining benign performance, we design a knowledge decoupling strategy with entropy-based weight assignment, encouraging poisoned samples to flow through the honeypot while guiding the main network to focus on benign learning. In addition, we introduce an automatic shortcut generation strategy to improve generalization across model architectures. Extensive experiments on four benchmark datasets and five model architectures demonstrate that our approach effectively mitigates a wide range of backdoor attacks while preserving performance on benign data. Code: https://github.com/Zixuan-Zhu/TR}{github.com/Zixuan-Zhu/TR.

</details>


### [69] [An Embedded RISC-V Evaluation of Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks in Hard-Constrained Recurrent Physics-Informed Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00737)
*Enzo Nicolas Spotorno,Josafat Leal Filho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了KAN与MLP作为HRPINN残差分支时，在RISC-V嵌入式平台（RV64GC）上的部署性能差异。结果显示，在精度可比的配置下，KAN比MLP慢4.7-14.5倍、能耗高4.7-18.7倍，且在INT8量化下轨迹发散时间早最多43倍，证明KAN的参数效率无法转化为嵌入式标量核上的部署效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明KAN在HRPINN残差分支中可能达到或优于MLP的发现精度且参数效率更高，但其可学习的B样条激活函数具有完全不同的执行模式。本文想探究这种参数效率在真实部署场景下是否依然成立，即能否转化为更低的延迟、更少的能耗和更好的可靠性。

Method: 在StarFive VisionFive 2（SiFive U74，RISC-V RV64GC，无向量扩展）平台上，使用相同训练权重，对KAN和MLP残差分支进行对比评测。测量了闭环递归积分器中每个积分步的执行延迟、能耗，以及在INT8后训练量化下的可靠性（轨迹发散时间）。测试覆盖了4个参数匹配的规模层级和2组精度可比对。

Result: 在精度可比的配置下，KAN残差分支比MLP慢8.0-13.5倍，能耗高5.6-11.3倍（最小配置3.7μJ对比0.33μJ）。所有4个规模层级上，KAN慢4.7-14.5倍，能耗高4.7-18.7倍。INT8量化下KAN轨迹比MLP早43倍发散，且损害可追溯到权重量化而非输入侧节点区间分配错误。

Conclusion: KAN在论文中报道的参数效率无法转化为嵌入式标量核心上的部署成本优势。对于嵌入式HRPINN部署，MLP残差分支是更可靠的默认选择，除非进行专门的量化协同设计。

Abstract: Hard-constrained recurrent physics-informed networks (HRPINNs) embed known dynamics inside a recurrent numerical integrator and restrict a neural branch to learning only the residual dynamics that the first-principles model does not capture. Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks (KANs) have been proposed as parameter-efficient replacements for multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) in such residual branches, but their learnable B-spline activations follow a markedly different execution profile. Building on prior work that characterized when a vanilla B-spline KAN matches or underperforms an MLP as an HRPINN residual branch in discovery accuracy, this paper asks whether that parameter efficiency survives deployment. Using identical trained weights, we measured execution latency, energy per integration step, and dependability under post-training quantization in the closed recurrent loop on a RISC-V RV64GC platform without vector extensions (StarFive VisionFive~2, SiFive U74). For the two accuracy-comparable pairs, the KAN residual branch executed $13.5\times$ and $8.0\times$ slower and consumed $11.3\times$ and $5.6\times$ more energy per integration step (3.7\,$μ$J against 0.33\,$μ$J for the smallest pair); across all four parameter-matched size tiers the ranges are $4.7\times$--$14.5\times$ and $4.7\times$--$18.7\times$. Under INT8 quantization, KAN trajectories diverged up to $43\times$ earlier than matched MLPs; the damage traces to weight quantization, not to input-side knot-interval misassignment. These results indicate that the parameter efficiency reported for KANs does not transfer to deployment cost on scalar embedded cores, and that an MLP residual branch is the more dependable default for embedded HRPINN deployment unless specific quantization co-design is used.

</details>


### [70] [Generic Vision and Cross-Attention for Reaction Yield Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00776)
*Qiwei Han,Chi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种双模态视觉交叉注意力架构（Vision Cross-Attention），融合表格物理有机数据与2D分子拓扑结构进行反应产率预测。实验表明，仅使用通用计算机视觉骨干处理2D骨架结构即可超越基于量子描述符的基线，而双模态融合的交叉注意力框架进一步取得最优性能（Test RMSE = 5.27%）。机制探针实验发现该网络通过视觉路径进行空间位阻识别，并为关键位阻瓶颈（如芳基卤化物）分配更高优先级，同时利用残差跳跃连接保护非空间电子参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统反应产率预测依赖一维量子描述符，缺乏显式的空间信息，限制了预测精度与可解释性。论文旨在通过引入二维分子拓扑的视觉信息填补这一空白，探索将视觉深度学习与物理化学相结合的新路径。

Method: 提出双模态Vision Cross-Attention架构，将表格类物理有机数据与2D分子骨架结构在交叉注意力机制下进行融合。使用通用CV骨干网络处理简单2D骨架结构，同时保留传统描述符通道，通过交叉注意力实现模态间信息交互，并采用残差跳跃连接保护电子参数。

Result: 单独使用通用视觉骨干处理2D骨架结构即可优于纯量子描述符基线；双模态融合的交叉注意力框架达到Test RMSE = 5.27%，优于传统方法。机制探针显示网络能动态学习化学层级，优先关注关键位阻瓶颈（芳基卤化物），并将宏观位阻识别卸载至视觉通路。

Conclusion: 该工作为物理化学预测提供了可扩展且高可解释性的深度学习蓝图，验证了视觉信息作为空间补充的可行性，为反应产率预测及其他物理化学任务融合深度视觉学习开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Traditional reaction yield prediction is constrained by 1D quantum descriptors that lack explicit spatial information. To address this gap, a dual-modal Vision Cross-Attention architecture is proposed, fusing tabular physical-organic data with 2D molecular topologies. Notably, it is demonstrated that a generic computer vision backbone processing simple 2D skeletal structures independently outperforms purely quantum-based baselines. By synergizing both modalities, superior predictive accuracy compared to traditional methodologies is achieved by the optimal cross-attention framework (Test RMSE = 5.27%). Through mechanistic probing, active, descriptor-guided spatial querying is observed, effectively offloading macroscopic steric identification to the visual pathway. Furthermore, a dynamic chemical hierarchy is learned by the network to heavily prioritize critical steric bottlenecks, such as the aryl halide. Concurrently, residual skip connections are utilized to protect non-spatial electronic parameters from destructive attenuation during fusion. Collectively, a scalable and highly interpretable blueprint is provided for augmenting physical chemistry with deep visual learning.

</details>


### [71] [Paris as a 15-Minute City: An Explainable AI Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00815)
*András J. Molnáar,Csaba I. Sidló,Rita Rónai,Domonkos Rózsay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用巴黎大都会区约7万条出行轨迹数据，结合POI和人口统计数据，构建步行和骑行可达性指标，研究15分钟城市理念与实际出行行为的关系。研究发现更高的本地服务可用性与更少的私人机动出行和更多的主动出行相关，但这一关系在外围区域明显减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 15分钟城市理念主张居民可在短距离步行或骑行内获得日常服务，但其与实际出行行为的关系难以量化，缺乏基于大规模实际轨迹数据的实证验证。

Method: 使用NetMob 2025数据挑战的移动轨迹数据（约7万条出行段），结合INSEE人口统计数据和OpenStreetMap POI数据，构建步行和骑行可达性指标。采用梯度提升树模型结合可解释机器学习（XAI）方法分析出行时长、交通方式及短途驾车行为的影响因素。

Result: 更高的POI可达性与更少的私人机动出行和更多主动出行相关，但这一关系在外围集聚区显著减弱。出行目的、家-工作距离、本地服务可达性、车辆保有量、公共交通订阅和社会人口背景是重要预测因素。高POI密度与短途出行中更低的驾车使用相关，而车辆保有量和驾照则与更高的驾车预测相关。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持15分钟城市的核心假设，同时揭示了显著的空间和人口异质性，表明可解释机器学习方法能够补充传统可达性指标，为城市出行政策提供本地化相关假设。

Abstract: The 15-minute city promotes access to everyday services within a short walk or bicycle ride, but its relationship with observed mobility remains difficult to quantify. We investigate this relationship in the Paris metropolitan area using mobility trajectories from the NetMob 2025 Data Challenge, enriched with INSEE sociodemographic data and OpenStreetMap points of interest (POIs), yielding approximately 70,000 trip segments after stop-based segmentation and data cleaning.
  We construct walking- and cycling-based indicators of local service availability and examine their associations with trip duration, transport mode, and short-trip car use. Higher POI availability is associated with less private motorized travel and more active mobility, although this relationship is substantially weaker in the outer agglomeration. Gradient-boosted tree models interpreted with explainable machine-learning methods consistently identify trip purpose, home--work distance, local service availability, vehicle ownership, public-transport subscription, and sociodemographic context as important predictors. For short trips, high POI density is associated with lower car use, while car ownership and driving-licence availability are associated with higher predicted car use; where services are sparse, public-transport subscription is associated with lower predicted car dependence. Finally, explainable AI (XAI) methods are used to examine how feature attributions change under alternative assumed variable orderings.
  The results are consistent with central assumptions of the 15-minute city while revealing substantial spatial and demographic heterogeneity. They also demonstrate how explainable machine-learning methods can complement accessibility indicators and identify locally relevant hypotheses for urban-mobility policy.

</details>


### [72] [AdvPlan-Bench: Adversarial Evaluation of Structured Plan-Generation Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00832)
*Alina Kapanova,Arun Kanhai,Natan Vidra,Spurthi Setty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AdvPlan-Bench，一个用于对抗性评估结构化计划生成代理的离线基准，通过类型化计划、对抗响应集、选择器诊断和可追踪的前沿指标来评估计划在对手可搜索响应时的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计划生成代理的评估往往将计划质量视为孤立属性，但现实规划任务中代理需要面对其他代理搜索响应的对抗性场景，缺乏针对此类对抗性评估的基准工具。

Method: 将计划表示为带可选分支的类型化动作链，分配合成质量分数，使用BLUE-vs-RED优势和Nash-gap诊断比较对抗计划，通过透明启发式规则评估定性约束一致性。包含150个场景、5种计划模板，以及多种策略对比和多评分者一致性研究。

Result: 采样的最佳响应策略（8个候选）将BLUE优势从.518降至.486，胜率从.900降至.820；离线LLM策略合约基线达到.496优势/.700胜率；两阶段多智能体理事会达到.509优势/.813胜率；600条评分记录的三评分者研究获得.978的评分者间一致性。

Conclusion: AdvPlan-Bench是一个可复现的基准工件，用于研究对抗性计划评估、响应预算敏感性、候选前沿和多智能体批判修订轨迹，但并非可操作规划器，也不提供关于真实世界决策质量的证据。

Abstract: Structured plan-generation agents are often evaluated as if a plan has quality in isolation, yet many realistic planning tasks require asking how a candidate behaves when another agent can search for responses. We introduce AdvPlan-Bench, an offline benchmark for adversarial evaluation of structured plan-generation agents. The contribution is a general evaluation object: a typed plan, an adversarial response set, selector diagnostics, and traceable candidate-frontier metrics. AdvPlan-Bench represents plans as typed action chains with optional branches, assigns synthetic quality scores, compares opposing plans with BLUE-vs-RED advantage and Nash-gap diagnostics, and evaluates qualitative constraint coherence with a transparent heuristic rubric. In 150 synthetic scenarios spanning five planning templates, a sampled best-response policy that draws eight response candidates reduces BLUE advantage from .518 to .486 and BLUE win rate from .900 to .820 relative to a single-sample response. An offline LLM-policy contract baseline reaches .496 BLUE advantage and .700 BLUE win rate, while a two-stage multi-agent council obtains .509 BLUE advantage and .813 BLUE win rate. A three-rater rubric-sensitivity study over 600 rating records yields .978 inter-rater agreement. AdvPlan-Bench is not an operational planner and provides no evidence about real-world decision quality; it is a reproducible benchmark artifact for studying adversarial plan evaluation, response-budget sensitivity, candidate frontiers, and multi-agent critique-and-revision traces.

</details>


### [73] [Deep Learning CNN and Recurrence Analysis for Alpha Gamma EEG Biomarkers in Fragile X Syndrome](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00835)
*Zag ElSayed,Payton Siekierski,Jack Yanchen Liu,Ernest Pedapati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多表征深度学习框架，通过整合CNN、LSTM和递归图分析，对脆性X综合征（FXS）患者的脑电图（EEG）α和γ频带信号进行自动化表型表征。实验表明，混合CNN-LSTM模型优于单一模态基线，其中γ特征判别力强，α与γ整合效果最佳，为FXS的EEG生物标志物开发提供了可扩展的途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FXS是一种神经发育障碍，与FMRP蛋白表达减少有关，导致突触可塑性异常、皮层过度兴奋和网络同步受损。EEG能无创揭示这些机制，且α和γ振荡异常与抑制控制、感觉处理和认知相关，但缺乏自动化的FXS EEG表型表征方法。

Method: 提出多表征深度学习框架：将限带EEG信号分解为α和γ分量，转化为多种互补表征（时间特征序列、时频图、编码非线性递归结构的递归图图像）；CNN模块学习图像表征中的空间-频谱和动态纹理特征，LSTM模块建模振荡活动的时间调制；混合CNN-LSTM架构联合捕获空间、时间和非线性依赖关系。

Result: 采用主体独立评估，混合模型在性能上优于单一模态基线；γ特征提供强判别力，α与γ特征整合达到最佳整体性能。

Conclusion: 该研究支持将深度学习与非线性表征相结合作为FXS EEG生物标志物开发的可扩展方法，在诊断、分层和治疗监测等转化应用方面具有潜在价值。

Abstract: Fragile X Syndrome (FXS) is a neurodevelopmental disorder caused by reduced expression of fragile X mental retardation protein (FMRP), leading to disrupted synaptic plasticity, cortical hyperexcitability, and impaired network synchronization. Electroencephalography (EEG) provides a noninvasive window into these mechanisms and consistently reveals abnormalities in alpha (8 to 12 Hz) and gamma (30 to 100 Hz) oscillations that relate to inhibitory control, sensory processing, and cognition. This paper proposes a multi representation deep learning framework for automated characterization of FXS EEG phenotypes by integrating convolutional neural networks (CNNs), long short-term memory (LSTM) networks, and recurrence plot (RP) analysis. Band limited EEG signals are decomposed into alpha and gamma components and transformed into complementary representations, including temporal feature sequences, time frequency maps, and RP images encoding the nonlinear recurrence structure. CNN modules learn discriminative spatial-spectral and dynamical textures from image based representations, while LSTM modules model temporal modulation of oscillatory activity; a hybrid CNN LSTM architecture jointly captures spatial, temporal, and nonlinear dependencies. Subject-independent evaluation demonstrates that the hybrid model outperforms single modality baselines, with gamma features providing strong discriminative power and alpha gamma integration yielding the best overall performance. These findings support deep learning with nonlinear representations as a scalable approach for EEG biomarker development in FXS, with potential utility for diagnosis, stratification, and treatment monitoring in translational settings.

</details>


### [74] [Nonlinear Laplacians Improve Signed-Directed Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00836)
*Ali Parviz,Yuichi Yoshida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向有符号且有向网络的非线性拉普拉斯算子（NLSD），并基于该算子构建了高效的谱图神经网络框架（NLSD-GNN），在节点分类和链接预测任务上取得了优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要使用线性拉普拉斯算子设计面向有符号有向图的图神经网络，但针对此类网络的非线性拉普拉斯算子研究相对缺乏。

Method: 提出非线性拉普拉斯算子NLSD，该算子扩展到有符号图的符号拉普拉斯和有向图的拉普拉斯概念；它根据节点特征计算节点特定的势能，仅当势能差异与边方向一致时才传递消息（反之忽略），从而利用与边方向对齐的势能差异进行消息传递；基于该算子构建谱GNN框架NLSD-GNN。

Result: 在节点分类和链接预测任务上进行了全面评估，涵盖有符号、有向或两者兼有的场景，结果表明NLSD-GNN能有效整合有符号和有向数据，并在多个数据集上取得优越性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的谱GNN框架不仅有效集成了有符号和有向信息，还在多样化数据集上实现了优于现有方法的性能表现。

Abstract: While signed-directed graphs have been studied using linear Laplacians in the design of graph neural networks, relatively little research has focused on developing non-linear Laplacian operators for such networks. We introduce a non-linear Laplacian operator specific to signed and directed networks (NLSD). This non-linear operator extends the concepts of the signed Laplacian for signed graphs and the Laplacian for directed graphs. The NLSD calculates node-specific potentials based on features More precisely, if the potential discrepancy is not aligned with the edge direction, we ignore it (and vice versa) leveraging message-passing techniques only across edges where potential discrepancies align with the edge's direction. Utilizing this novel operator, we propose an efficient spectral GNN framework (NLSD-GNN). We conducted comprehensive evaluations focusing on node classification and link prediction, examining scenarios involving signed, directional, or both types of information. Our findings reveal that this spectral GNN framework not only integrates signed and directional data effectively but also achieves superior performance across diverse datasets.

</details>

### [75] [Track-Guided Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Vehicle Drifting with Minimum-Lap-Time Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00113)
*Sheng Zhao,Bolin Zhao,Xiaodong Wu,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向最小圈时（MLT）漂移场景的规划-控制框架，通过最优控制问题生成MLT漂移规划轨迹作为先验数据，并提出了Track-guided强化学习（TgRL）方法，分阶段训练漂移控制策略、漂移过弯策略和综合漂移比赛策略，仿真结果表明该框架能在保证车辆运动控制性能的同时有效缩短圈时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在拉力赛中，驾驶员通过主动打破轮胎抓地力来进行漂移，以快速调整车辆姿态缩短圈时。自动驾驶漂移是一个复杂的双目标控制问题：既要稳定高度非线性的漂移动力学，又要严格最小化圈时，这对现有的控制架构提出了挑战。

Method: 首先构建最优控制问题生成MLT漂移规划轨迹作为先验数据；然后提出Track-guided Reinforcement Learning（TgRL）漂移控制方法，采用渐进式三步训练策略：从漂移控制策略→漂移过弯策略→综合漂移比赛策略；奖励函数包含即时奖励项和基于MLT目标的终点奖励项。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的框架使智能体能够学习到漂移比赛策略，既能保证车辆运动控制性能，又能有效减少圈时。

Conclusion: 本文提出的规划-控制框架和TgRL方法有效解决了自动漂移中稳定控制与最小圈时优化的双重目标问题，验证了渐进式强化学习训练策略在高度非线性漂移动力学学习中的可行性。

Abstract: In Formula 1, drivers optimize racing lines within tire grip limits to minimize lap times; however, in rally racing, drivers intentionally break traction to drift on loose surfaces. This maneuver rapidly aligns the vehicle for corner exits, ultimately reducing lap time. Autonomously executing such maneuvers formulates a complex dual-objective control problem: stabilizing highly nonlinear drift dynamics while strictly minimizing lap time. Addressing this challenge motivates the development of advanced Minimum-Lap-Time (MLT) drift control architectures. This paper proposes a planning-control framework specifically designed for MLT drifting scenario. First, we formulate an optimal control problem to generate a MLT drift planning trajectory, which is used as prior data to train a deep reinforcement learning drift controller. Given that drifting involves extremely large sideslip angles and is therefore challenging to learn directly, a Track-guided Reinforcement Learning (TgRL) drift control method is proposed to enable progressive training in a step-by-step manner, from drift control policy, to drift corner policy, and finally to a comprehensive drift race policy. The reward function incorporates both an instant reward term and an end reward term derived from the Minimum-Lap-Time objective. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed framework enables the agent to learn a drift racing policy that not only ensures vehicle motion control performance but also effectively reduces lap time.

</details>


### [76] [Motion Planning for Mobile Manipulators Navigating Doorways via Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00206)
*Kasra Sinaei,Kasun Weerakoon,Christopher Bradley,Seyed Abolfazl Fakoorian,Donald Ebeigbe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于非线性模型预测控制（MPC）的运动规划框架，用于移动机械臂自主开门并穿越推门和拉门。通过将机器人与门建模为耦合动态系统，并采用基于惩罚的约束来保证操作可行性，该方法在仿真和硬件实验中均成功规划出动态可行且无碰撞的轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 穿越门廊是移动机械臂在人类环境中运行的基本能力，需要移动基座与机械臂之间的协调运动。然而，现有的运动规划方法在实现自主开门与穿越方面仍面临挑战，因此需要一种能够生成动态可行且无碰撞轨迹的规划框架。

Method: 该方法将机器人与门构建为耦合动态系统，并将其纳入非线性模型预测控制（MPC）优化框架中。通过引入基于惩罚的约束来强制执行操作可行性，从而避免了在规划器中显式建模机械臂运动学。

Result: 仿真实验和硬件实验均表明，所提出的方法能够成功规划出穿越门廊（包括推门和拉门）的可行轨迹。

Conclusion: 该MPC-based运动规划框架为移动机械臂的自主门廊穿越提供了一种有效解决方案，通过惩罚约束简化了机械臂建模，并在实际环境中验证了其可行性。

Abstract: Navigating doorways is a fundamental capability for mobile manipulators operating in human environments, requiring coordinated motion between the mobile base and manipulator arm. This paper presents a motion planning framework that generates dynamically feasible and collision-free trajectories for autonomously opening and traversing both push and pull doors. The proposed method formulates the robot and door as a coupled dynamical system within a nonlinear Model Predictive Control (MPC) optimization framework. Manipulation feasibility is enforced through a penalty-based constraint, avoiding explicit arm kinematic modeling in the planner. Simulations and a hardware experiment demonstrate that the approach successfully plans feasible trajectories for door traversal.

</details>


### [77] [Developing Combined Manipulation and Locomotion Skills with Interaction Representation and Skill Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00208)
*Fanxing Meng,Jing Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于发展心理学原理的人形机器人策略学习方法，包括学习全身抓取策略，并将其与站起和行走策略组合成更复杂的操作与运动策略。实验显示对未见物体的零样本抓取成功率达93%，持物站起成功率达96-100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人需要学习复杂的行为策略，但现有方法通常依赖外部数据集或预训练模型。作者希望基于发展心理学中的发育原理，让机器人自主地、逐步地学习通用抓取策略，并探索如何将不同策略组合成更复杂的行为。

Method: (1) 借鉴谐波分析，采用三次谐波作为权重，通过空间卷积表示手-物空间关系，并结合基于发育原理的帧内手指关节解耦课程，使机器人自主学习通用抓取策略。(2) 将抓取策略与独立学习的起身策略组合，为每个策略提供各自的观测向量，并使用手-物交互分数决定各策略何时控制哪些机器人关节。

Result: 对未见物体的零样本抓取成功率为93%，持物站立成功率为96-100%。同时发现，策略组合只有在各策略在同一整个人形身体上学习时才有效，即使某些策略（如运动策略）看似不需要所有身体部位（如手指）。

Conclusion: 基于发育原理的方法可以使机器人自主学习通用抓取策略，并成功地将不同策略组合成更复杂的行为。策略组合的有效性依赖于各策略在完整的同一人形身体上进行学习，这一发现对未来人形机器人策略学习与组合具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: This paper addresses how to enable a humanoid robot to learn motion policies based on developmental principles and combine policies to create more sophisticated and useful behaviors. Specifically, we present an approach to (1) learning a whole-body reaching and grasping policy and (2) combining it and a standing-up and walking policy to compose a more complex policy of manipulation and locomotion: grasping, standing up, and walking.
  In (1), our method draws inspiration from harmonic analysis and adopts cubic harmonics as weights to represent the hand-object spatial relationship via spatial convolution. Utilizing an intra-episode finger joint decoupling curriculum based on developmental principles, a robot can autonomously learn a generalizable grasping policy without relying on external datasets or pretrained models.
  In (2), our method combines the grasping policy with a separately learned getting-up policy by providing both policies with their respective observation vectors and using hand-object interaction scores to determine when each policy should control which robot joints. Our results show a 93% zero-shot success rate for grasping unseen objects and a 96-100% success rate for standing up while holding the object. Our work also demonstrates that combining different policies is only effective if each policy learning happens on the same whole humanoid body even if a policy (such as for locomotion) does not seem to need all the body parts (such as fingers).

</details>


### [78] [Compliant Sphere Lattice Contact: Distributed Contact Modeling for Sphere-Based Robot Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00263)
*Nataliya Nechyporenko,Ava Abderezaei,Alessandro Roncone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CSLC（Compliant Sphere Lattice Contact），一种基于球体表示的分布式接触模型，通过将机器人界面建模为带有锚定弹簧和侧向弹簧的柔性球体晶格，在球体表示上实现接触斑块的形成，从而在保持计算效率的同时提升物理精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球体机器人表示虽然计算高效、支持快速碰撞检测和可微几何，但由于依赖点接触，无法捕捉接触斑块面积、压力分布、旋转刚度和摩擦力矩等物理特性，在物理精度上存在不足。

Method: 将机器人接触界面建模为柔性球体晶格，球体之间通过锚定弹簧和侧向弹簧连接；当与物体接触时晶格发生变形，产生空间分布的接触斑块，从而在球体表示框架内提升接触的物理精度。

Result: 在两个独立求解器上验证了CSLC的有效性，初步结果显示该方法能够形成接触斑块并提升抓取稳定性。

Conclusion: CSLC在保留球体表示计算优势的同时，通过分布式接触建模弥补了点接触模型的物理精度缺陷，为接触规划提供了更准确的模型基础。

Abstract: Contact planning in robotics requires models that are both computationally efficient and physically accurate. Sphere-based robot representations satisfy the first requirement by enabling fast collision checking and differentiable geometry, but sacrifice physical accuracy by relying on point contact which cannot capture contact patch area, pressure distributions, rotational stiffness, or frictional moments. We introduce Compliant Sphere Lattice Contact (CSLC), a distributed contact model that operates natively on sphere representations by modeling the robot interface as a compliant lattice of surface spheres connected through anchor and lateral springs. When pressed against an object, the lattice deforms to produce a spatially distributed contact patch that improves the physical accuracy of sphere-based contact. We validate CSLC across two independent solvers and show preliminary results demonstrating contact patch formation and improved grasp stability.

</details>


### [79] [Localization in Spatiotemporal Fields via Environmental PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00272)
*Jose Fuentes,Abdullah Al Redwan Newaz,Ana Cavalcanti,Leonardo Bobadilla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用偏微分方程（PDE）控制时空场作为定位签名的定位框架，涵盖浅水方程和扩散方程两类PDE。采用Rao-Blackwellized粒子滤波器（RBPF）将车辆状态分解为非线性部分和线性传感器偏差部分，分别用粒子采样和逐粒子卡尔曼滤波处理，以减少所需粒子数并处理传感器漂移。仿真和现场实验均验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统定位方法（如GPS）在特定环境中可能受限，而自然环境中的物理场（如水温、盐度、溶解氧等）可作为天然定位信号。作者旨在利用PDE建模这些环境场，为无人水面艇等自主系统提供无需外部基础设施的定位方案，同时解决传感器漂移问题和降低粒子滤波器的计算成本。

Method: 1) 选取两类PDE：浅水方程（描述自由表面流）和扩散方程（描述标量传输与混合）作为环境场模型；2) 用数值PDE求解器预测全域场，将多个场通道作为多模态测量融合；3) 采用Rao-Blackwellized粒子滤波器（RBPF），将车辆状态分为非线性部分（粒子采样）和线性传感器偏差部分（逐粒子卡尔曼滤波）；4) 通过仿真和无人水面艇现场实验（测量盐度、温度和溶解氧）进行验证。

Result: 仿真实验表明，在两个PDE场景中，RBPF在最终位置误差和均方根误差（RMSE）方面均优于标准粒子滤波器，且在较少的粒子数下表现更佳。现场实验验证了PDE控制的环境场具有足够的空间变异性，可用于实际定位。

Conclusion: PDE驱动的环境场可以作为一种有效的定位签名，RBPF框架在减少计算开销的同时能有效处理传感器漂移，显著提升定位精度。该方法为GPS受限环境下的自主系统定位提供了新的可行方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a localization framework that uses spatiotemporal fields governed by partial differential equations (PDEs) as localization signatures. Two PDE classes are considered: the shallow water equations, which describe free-surface flows in coastal and riverine environments, and the advection-diffusion equation, which models the transport and mixing of scalar quantities such as temperature, salinity, and dissolved oxygen. A numerical PDE solver provides predicted fields over the domain, and multiple field channels are fused as multimodal measurements to improve localization accuracy. We formulate the problem within a Rao-Blackwellized particle filter (RBPF) that partitions the vehicle state into a nonlinear component sampled by particles and a linear sensor bias component tracked analytically via per-particle Kalman filters. This factorization reduces the required number of particles compared to a standard particle filter while accounting for realistic sensor drift. Simulation studies on both PDE scenarios show that the RBPF consistently outperforms a standard particle filter in terms of final position error and Root Mean Square Error (RMSE) across varying particle counts. Field experiments with an autonomous surface vehicle measuring salinity, temperature, and dissolved oxygen validate that PDE-governed environmental fields provide sufficient spatial variability for practical localization. Related experimental videos are available at https://localization-environmental-pdes.github.io/.

</details>


### [80] [Hybrid Attention Estimation Pipeline for Adaptive HRI Using an Expressive Robotic Head](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00284)
*Pablo Moraes,Monica Rodriguez,Christopher Peters,Hiago Sodre,Tobias Doernbach,Bruna Guterres,Ricardo Grando*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于InMoov表情机器人头部的混合视觉注意力估计方法，用于人机交互。该方法结合快速几何感知层（高频率面部和头部姿态信息）与基于视觉语言模型的语义感知层（生成情境化注意力标签），通过有限状态机整合信号并调节自适应交互行为。10名参与者、40次试验验证了系统在交互启动、暂停行为和语义信息非冗余性方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机交互中，机器人需要准确估计人类注意力状态以实现自然流畅的交互。现有方法通常依赖单一感知方式，几何方法虽快但对情境理解有限，纯语义方法速度不足。因此需要一种混合方案来兼顾实时性和语义理解能力。

Method: 提出一个双层级联管线：(1) 快速几何感知层提供高频面部检测和头部姿态估计，用于时序调控；(2) 独立的语义感知层接收原始第一人称摄像头帧，基于视觉语言模型生成上下文注意力标签（关注机器人、使用手机、注意力分散）。两者通过有限状态机整合，调节机器人交互行为（激活、等待、交互恢复、回归静止）。

Result: 在10名参与者、40次试验中（涵盖基线和自适应交互条件），系统在所有试验中均实现了可靠的交互启动，在自适应分心条件下表现出一致的暂停行为，且几何层与语义层的输出之间语义信息具有非冗余性。

Conclusion: 该混合视觉注意力估计系统能够有效支持人机交互中的自适应行为调节，验证了几何感知与语义感知结合的技术路线在实际机器人平台上的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: This paper presents an applied case study on hybrid visual attention estimation for human-robot interaction using an expressive robotic head based on the InMoov ecosystem. The proposed pipeline combines a fast geometric perception layer with an independent semantic perception layer based on a vision-language model. The geometric layer provides high-frequency face and head-pose information for temporal regulation, while the semantic layer receives only raw egocentric camera frames and produces contextual attention labels related to attention toward the robot, phone use, or attention elsewhere. These signals are integrated through a finite state machine that regulates adaptive interaction behavior, including activation, waiting, interaction resumption, and return to rest. The system was evaluated with 10 participants across 40 trials covering baseline and adaptive interaction conditions. Results show reliable interaction start across all trials, consistent pause behavior in the adaptive distraction condition, and non-redundant semantic information between the geometric and semantic outputs.

</details>


### [81] [Towards General Language-Conditioned Latent Safety Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00315)
*Ihab Tabbara,Yuxuan Yang,Hussein Sibai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种语言条件化的安全过滤器，将Hamilton-Jacobi安全actor和critic以语言指定的约束为条件，使机器人策略能够在任务变化的同时适应不同安全要求，实验表明该方法能减少约束违反并具有一定的跨约束实例迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的安全过滤器大多是约束特定的，当安全要求随用户、环境或应用场景变化时需要重新设计或重新学习，缺乏灵活性。因此需要一种能够根据语言指定的约束条件自适应调整的安全过滤机制。

Method: 提出语言条件化安全过滤框架，将Hamilton-Jacobi安全actor和critic以语言指定的约束为条件进行训练，在视觉场景下应用于拾放、擦桌和堆叠积木等任务，评估其执行语言约束的能力及对未见约束实例的泛化能力。

Result: 实验结果表明，语言条件化的安全过滤器能够减少约束违反，并在所评估的约束族内对未见约束实例表现出部分的迁移能力。

Conclusion: 语言条件化安全过滤是提升VLA策略安全部署灵活性的有效方向，但跨约束实例的泛化仍有提升空间，值得进一步研究。

Abstract: Robot policies are becoming increasingly general, with vision-language-action (VLA) models enabling a single policy to execute diverse tasks specified in natural language. Safe deployment, however, requires adapting not only to new tasks but also to varying safety requirements across users, environments, and applications. Existing safety filters remain largely constraint-specific and thus must be redesigned or relearned when safety requirements change. In this paper, we investigate language-conditioned safety filtering, in which a Hamilton-Jacobi safety actor and critic are conditioned on language-specified constraints. We evaluate this formulation across pick-and-place, table-wiping, and block-stacking tasks in the vision-based setting, examining its ability to enforce language-specified constraints and transfer to unseen constraint instances within the evaluated constraint families. Our experiments provide evidence that language-conditioned safety filters reduce constraint violations and exhibit partial transfer to unseen constraint instances.

</details>


### [82] [Belief-Space Perception Routing under Coupled Sensor Faults and Compute Contention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00322)
*Sparsh Roy,Vihan Aggarwal,Davin Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种感知路由器，它联合跟踪传感器故障状态与计算争用状态的概率估计（通过noisy-OR项耦合），并在YOLO11x/n和不同分辨率配置间选择，以确保帧在截止时间前完成。实验表明，当两种压力源共同出现时，耦合策略相比独立处理策略将截止时间未命中率降低1.1至9.4个百分点；但野外数据中未发现耦合现象的普遍存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在固定时钟节拍下进行视觉感知面临双重问题：相机在雨、泥、雾和黑暗中性能退化，同时单一机载处理器被规划和控制共享，留给感知的计算资源每秒都在波动。现有系统大多分别建模这两个问题，缺乏联合考虑。

Method: 提出一个感知路由器，跟踪传感器故障状态和计算争用状态的概率估计，通过noisy-OR项将两者耦合，利用耦合估计在四种检测器配置（YOLO11x/n，1280或640像素）中选择，以保证帧在截止时间前完成。

Result: 两种压力源共同出现时，耦合策略比独立策略降低截止时间未命中率1.1至9.4个百分点；六个条件中五个的置信区间排除零，10个序列6个条件的合并效应符号检验p=0.001，所有非耦合对照和故障-free轨迹为0.0pp。路由开销仅数十微秒/帧。但在8个真实RADIATE恶劣天气序列和3个工作量代理上，经Benjamini-Hochberg校正和重复运行后，24个测试均未发现耦合现象存在的证据。

Conclusion: 耦合策略在仿真/受控条件下被证明是有效的机制证明，但野外数据中未观察到耦合现象的普遍存在，该问题仍然开放。作者发布了评估流水线，供部署方用自身轨迹验证耦合是否存在。

Abstract: A robot that has to see and react on a fixed clock runs into two problems at once. Its cameras degrade in rain, mud, fog, and darkness. And the single onboard processor it runs on is shared with planning and control, so the compute left over for perception moves around from second to second. Most systems model the two separately. We present a perception router that tracks probabilistic estimates of sensor-fault state and compute- contention state, couples them with a noisy-OR term, and uses the coupled estimate to pick one of four detector configurations (YOLO11x/n at 1280 or 640 px) so that the frame finishes before its deadline. Where the two stressors co-occur, the coupled policy cuts the deadline-miss rate by 1.1 to 9.4 percentage points against a policy that treats them independently. The interval excludes zero in five of six conditions, the pooled effect over 10 sequences and 6 conditions has sign-test p = 0.001, and every uncoupled control and the fault-free trajectory sit at exactly 0.0 pp. Routing costs tens of microseconds per frame. We then asked whether the coupling the method exploits arises on its own. Across eight real RADIATE adverse-weather sequences and three workload proxies independent of the fault signal, after Benjamini-Hochberg correction and a replication run, none of 24 tests found it. We report that null and scope the routing result as a proof of mechanism. Whether such coupling occurs in the field is still open, and the released evaluation pipeline lets a deployment settle it on its own traces.

</details>


### [83] [Action Chunk Scheduling for Batched Robot Policy Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00337)
*Rohan Bansal,David He,Nadun Ranawaka Arachchige,Zhenyang Chen,Soobum Kim,Kexin Rong,Danfei Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Armory系统，解决从远程GPU为多台机器人服务机器人策略的调度问题，通过考虑动作块消费速率的异构性来优化调度，在实际实验中吞吐量提升最高达18%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础模型（如VLA）计算需求高，而设备端算力受功率和空间限制，如何高效地从远程GPU为多台机器人提供服务是规模化部署的关键挑战。

Method: 将机器人策略的多机器人远程服务问题形式化为调度问题，构建Armory服务系统，并提出一种考虑机器人动作块消费速率异构性的调度算法。

Result: 在模拟和真实机器人车队上验证，当所有机器人相同朴素调度表现良好，但当动作块消费速率不同时，所提算法在实际实验中提升系统吞吐量最高达18%。

Conclusion: 揭示了传统批处理方法与机器人策略执行闭环需求之间的不匹配，证明了考虑异构性的调度算法能有效提升多机器人远程策略服务的整体吞吐量。

Abstract: Deploying robot foundation models at scale is the next step towards realizing the potential of general-purpose robots. However, Vision-Language-Action (VLA) and other foundation models are computationally demanding, and on-device compute is constrained by power and space. In this paper, we introduce the problem of serving a robot policy to multiple robots from a remote GPU and formulate it as a scheduling problem. We build Armory, a serving system validated on fleets of both simulated and real robots. Our experiments show that naive scheduling heuristics perform well when all robots are the same, but fall short when robots consume action chunks at different rates, uncovering a mismatch between conventional batching methods and the closed-loop requirements of robot policy execution. To address this, we propose a scheduling algorithm that accounts for this heterogeneity and improves overall system throughput by up to $18\%$ in real-world experiments. Additional details are available at https://gatech-rl2.github.io/actionchunkscheduling.

</details>


### [84] [A Robotic System for Automated Manufacturing of Dielectric Elastomer Actuators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00369)
*Van Remenar,Anatol Gogoj,Ang Li,Tomas Verardi,Matei Mandoiu,Victor Jimenez Santiago,Mihai Duduta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于软电容（兼作执行器和传感器）的自动化机器人制造系统，重点优化了旋涂介电层和冲压碳纳米管电极两大关键工艺。该系统成功制造了20个多层介电弹性体执行器（DEA），每个包含12层介电层（平均厚度55.37±2.04 μm）和11层交替CNT电极，总工艺时间减少14.2%，并让操作员从56.1%的工序中解放出来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软电容作为执行器和传感器在软机器人领域具有重要应用价值，但其制造过程高度依赖人工操作，存在效率低、一致性差的问题。介电层形成和CNT电极施加是决定器件质量最关键的两个环节，亟需自动化来解决人工制造带来的瓶颈。

Method: 构建自动化机器人制造系统，通过旋涂法实现介电层成型，采用冲压法完成CNT电极施加，将这两项核心工艺集成到机器人系统中实现全自动化批量制造。共制造20个多层DEA器件，每个含12层介电层（平均厚度55.37±2.04 μm）和11层交替CNT电极。

Result: 系统成功制造20个多层DEA器件，各层厚度一致性良好（55.37±2.04 μm）。与人工制造相比，总工艺时间缩短14.2%，操作员参与时间减少了56.1%，显著提升了制造效率和自动化水平。

Conclusion: 该自动化机器人制造系统能够高效、一致地生产多层软电容执行器/传感器，在减少人工干预的同时提升了制造效率和器件质量一致性，为软电容的规模化生产提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This letter presents an automated robotic manufacturing system for soft capacitors which operate as actuators and sensors. Emphasis is placed on the two processes that most directly govern device quality, dielectric layer formation by spin coating and carbon nanotube (CNT) electrode application by stamping. Twenty multilayer DEAs, each comprising 12 dielectric layers with a mean thickness of 55.37 +- 2.04 um and 11 alternating CNT electrodes, were fabricated reducing total process time by 14.2% and removing the operator from 56.1% of it.

</details>


### [85] [The Gate, Not the Cache: Gate Provenance Bounds the Closed-Loop Reliability of Training-Free VLA Token Skipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00391)
*Qi Luo,Shuaijun Liu,Hao Zhao,Kunlin Li,Xiaobo Wang,Ningxing Su,Dongsheng Wang,Yun Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了VLA模型中token跳过加速方法的闭环可靠性问题，发现门控信号来源（而非跳过机制本身）决定了加速VLA的闭环可靠性。提出了actuation-slack refresh方法，在机器人执行动作块期间进行一次密集前向传播刷新门控和KV缓存，修复了自收获门控导致的性能崩溃，两个机制均恢复到0.98，服务延迟仍比密集基线低18-22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Token skipping可加速VLA模型，但当门控信号来自前一次加速前向传播时，跳过的token对下一步门控最不可见，损伤会跨控制步累积直至任务失败。现有评估中这种崩溃对动作级检测器不可见，且触发式刷新不可靠，因此需要无条件的密集刷新方案。

Method: 分析了reuse和deletion两种token跳过机制的门控信号来源影响，提出actuation-slack refresh：在机器人执行当前动作块期间（关键路径之外）执行一次密集前向传播，为下一步提供干净门控和新鲜KV基数。刷新是无条件的（而非触发式），因为现有检测器无法可靠发现失败。

Result: 在LIBERO-Object上，skip ratio 0.9时两种情况（废弃/重用）从自收获门控分别崩溃到0.31和0.68（密集为1.00）；采用刷新后两种机制均恢复到0.98。集成进SOTA缓存与剪枝方法后，跨2个VLA策略、4个LIBERO套件、4个SIMPLER任务修复了所有由自收获门控引起的崩溃，服务延迟比密集基线降低18-22%。

Conclusion: 门控信号来源（是否来自无跳过的密集前向传播）而非token跳过机制本身，决定了加速VLA的闭环可靠性。actuation-slack refresh通过在关键路径外进行密集刷新，同时保留了skipping的速度和dense pass的信息量。

Abstract: Token skipping is a widely used training-free way to accelerate vision--language--action (VLA) models by bypassing computation for most visual tokens at each control step according to a gate. When the next gate is harvested from the previous accelerated forward, however, the tokens skipped at one step are also the ones least visible to the next gate, and the damage can compound across control steps until the task fails. We study the two mechanisms this class is built on, reuse and deletion, crossing each against where its gate signal comes from on identical episodes. At a skip ratio of 0.9 on LIBERO-Object, both collapse when the gate comes from the model's own accelerated forwards, to 0.68 under reuse and to 0.31 under deletion against a dense 1.00, and the collapse is invisible to the action-level detectors we evaluate. What separates collapse from dense-level operation is not the mechanism but whether the gate is clean, computed by a forward that skipped nothing. We therefore propose actuation-slack refresh, one dense pass run while the robot executes its current action chunk, off the critical path, that hands the next step a clean gate and a fresh KV base. Since the measured detectors do not reliably reveal the failure, the refresh is unconditional rather than triggered. Both mechanisms then recover to 0.98, keeping the speed of skipping and the information of a dense pass. We then integrate the refresh into state-of-the-art caching and pruning methods across two VLA policies, 4 LIBERO suites, and 4 SIMPLER tasks, where it repairs every collapse caused by using a self-harvested gate. Serve latency drops 18--22\% below dense, measured both in simulation and on a physical robot. Where the gate signal comes from, not how tokens are skipped, decides closed-loop reliability for accelerated VLAs.

</details>


### [86] [Embodied Passive Aeroacoustic Perception Enables Relative Sensing and Pursuit Between Aerial Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00401)
*Yanbaihui Liu,Ravi Prakash,Li-Yu Lo,Nils Roede,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的感知范式——具身被动气动声学感知（embodied passive aeroacoustic perception），利用无人机飞行时自然产生的声音信号实现相对状态感知与追踪，无需主动声学信号、通信、GPS或外部基础设施。系统SonicFly通过四麦克风阵列、神经网络方位-距离估计和置信度门控滤波，在强自噪声干扰下实现了1.34 m的平均距离保持误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 飞行机器人产生的结构化气动声场信号尚未被充分探索作为机载相对感知的信息源，特别是在强自声干扰和环境多变条件下，现有感知方法依赖主动信号或外部设施，缺乏利用自身飞行声音进行被动感知的研究。

Method: SonicFly框架包含：轻量四麦克风阵列、旋翼机感知声学表征、神经方位-距离估计器（neural bearing-range estimator）以及置信度门控滤波（confidence-gated filtering）用于闭环飞行控制。系统仅利用领航无人机固有飞行声音实现追踪。

Result: 在多种户外轨迹和运行条件下，SonicFly在纯声学追踪中实现了1.34 m的平均距离保持误差。声学通道分析揭示了谐波结构、频谱可分离性和空间声学线索在可观测性中的作用。

Conclusion: 多旋翼气动声学信号即使在强自声干扰、环境变化和飞行几何改变的条件下仍包含足够的相对感知信息，证明了具身被动气动声学感知对空中机器人的可行性，并表明自然生成的行为信号可作为机器人感知与协调的信息来源。

Abstract: Aerial robots generate structured aeroacoustic fields during flight, yet these signals have been underexplored as a source of onboard relative perception, particularly under the strong ego-acoustic interference generated during simultaneous flight in various outdoor conditions. We introduce embodied passive aeroacoustic perception, a sensing paradigm in which an aerial robot infers actionable relative-state information from the naturally generated sound of flight while operating within its own evolving aeroacoustic field. We present SonicFly, a passive aeroacoustic perception framework that enables one unmanned aerial vehicle to estimate and follow another using only the leader's intrinsic flight sound, without active acoustic signaling, inter-robot communication, GPS sharing, or external sensing infrastructure. The system uses a lightweight four-microphone array, rotorcraft-informed acoustic representations, a neural bearing-range estimator, and confidence-gated filtering for closed-loop flight. Through acoustic characterization, onboard localization, and outdoor pursuit experiments, we show that multirotor aeroacoustic signals contain sufficient information to support relative perception despite strong ego-acoustic interference, environmental variability, and changing flight geometry. During acoustic-only pursuit, SonicFly achieved a mean distance-maintenance error of 1.34 m across diverse outdoor trajectories and operating conditions. Analysis of the acoustic channel reveals design principles governing embodied passive aeroacoustic perception, including the roles of harmonic structure, spectral separability, and spatial acoustic cues in determining observability. Our results establish the feasibility of embodied passive aeroacoustic perception for aerial robots and suggest that naturally generated behavioral signals can serve as information for robotic perception and coordination.

</details>


### [87] [Learning to Predict Contact Force Distributions from Vision Leveraging Object Geometry Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00464)
*Ryo Hanai,Yukiyasu Domaea,Ixchel G. Ramirez-Alpizar,Abdullah Mustafa,Floris Erich,Tetsuya Ogata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种从单张RGB图像预测三维力分布的方法，通过统计平滑模拟器输出点力并结合物体几何信息，使机器人能够像人类一样基于视觉先验做出物理预测。实验证明该方法在仿真和真实环境中均提升了预测精度和下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能够基于视觉和经验对物理交互进行粗略预测并调整操控策略，但现有模拟器只能输出带噪声的点力，无法实现一致的预测。作者希望通过预测平滑的力分布（而非原始点力）来提升机器人物理预测的准确性和稳定性。

Method: 采用刚体模拟器收集视觉与力的配对数据，提出构建从单张RGB图像预测三维力分布的模型。目标分布通过对模拟器输出的点力应用统计平滑生成，并在平滑过程中融入物体几何信息，以考虑接触状态的差异，实现更一致的基于视觉的预测。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中的大量评估表明：该方法提高了预测精度，平滑处理增强了下游任务性能，几何引导的平滑带来进一步收益。仅用仿真数据训练的模型能够有效泛化到真实世界场景。

Conclusion: 预测平滑力分布而非原始点力，结合物体几何引导的平滑，是提升机器人基于视觉的物理预测能力和下游操控性能的有效途径，且仿真到真实的迁移能力令人瞩目。

Abstract: Based on vision and prior experience, humans can make rough physical predictions and adjust their manipulation strategies. This paper aims to endow robots with a similar ability. To collect paired data of vision and forces, we use a rigid-body simulator commonly adopted in robotics. However, unlike simulators that output noisy point forces, humans are able to make consistent predictions even in unfamiliar situations. Based on this observation, we hypothesize that predicting smooth force distributions rather than raw point forces can improve both force prediction itself and downstream task performance. To validate this hypothesis, we construct a model that predicts three-dimensional force distributions from a single RGB image of piled daily objects. The target distribution is generated by applying statistical smoothing to point forces obtained from the simulator. Moreover, by incorporating object geometry into the smoothing process, we aim to account for variations in contact states and achieve more consistent vision-based predictions. We conduct extensive evaluations in both simulation and real environments. Results show that our approach improves prediction accuracy, enhances downstream task performance through smoothing, and further benefits from geometry-guided smoothing. Remarkably, the trained model generalizes effectively to real-world scenes despite being trained solely in simulation.

</details>


### [88] [From Digital to Physical Reservoir Computing: Co-Optimizing Soft Robotic Reservoirs via Dynamics Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00484)
*Nicola Visentin,Maximilian Stölzle,Mariano Ramírez Montero,Francesco Braghin,Daniela Rus,Cosimo Della Santina*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对软体机器人物理储备池计算（PRC）的动力学级联合优化框架，通过将物理储备池预测练到高性能数字参考动力学上，实现29.6%的平均性能提升，跨越多个分类和预测任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人物理储备池计算在非线性动力学、时间记忆和高维状态变换方面具有潜力，但传统上物理储备池未经预训练或协同优化，导致其性能相对于数字储备池可能受限。作者希望探索物理储备池是否可以针对高性能数字参考动力学进行预训练。

Method: 提出联合优化公式，同时优化物理参数、微分同胚物理-参考状态映射和反馈控制。使用可微物理模型和加速度级方程误差目标（避免时间积分），以模拟软体机器人为实例，以随机振荡器网络（RON）为参考动力学，并采用并行多起点梯度下降进行优化。

Result: 在sMNIST和ADIAC分类任务以及Mackey-Glass和Lorenz96预测任务上，跨四种储备池维度进行评估。优化后的储备池相比未经优化的软体机器人储备池，在所有任务和数据集上实现了33.7%的平均相对改进，且性能接近数字参考。

Conclusion: 研究证明了在模拟软体机器人储备池中，动力学级协同优化是可行的，能够显著提升物理储备池计算性能并接近数字参考水平。

Abstract: Soft robotic substrates are promising for Physical Reservoir Computing (PRC) because their compliant nonlinear dynamics can provide temporal memory, high-dimensional state transformations, and efficient inference. However, physical reservoirs are often adopted as-is rather than pretrained or co-optimized, potentially limiting soft robotic PRC performance relative to digital reservoirs. We investigate whether a physical reservoir can instead be pretrained against high-performing digital reference dynamics. Our formulation jointly optimizes physical parameters, a diffeomorphic physical-reference state map, and feedforward-feedback control using a differentiable physical model and an acceleration-level equation-error objective that avoids temporal integration. As a proof of concept, we instantiate the formulation with simulated soft robots, a Random Oscillators Network (RON) reference, and parallel multi-start gradient descent. We evaluate the optimized reservoirs on classification (sMNIST and ADIAC) and forecasting (Mackey-Glass and Lorenz96) tasks across four reservoir dimensions. Compared with unoptimized soft robot reservoirs, the optimized reservoirs achieve a mean relative improvement of 33.7% across all tasks and datasets, while remaining close to the digital reference. These results demonstrate the feasibility of dynamics-level co-optimization for the simulated soft robotic reservoirs considered here.

</details>


### [89] [First Deployable Dynamic-CoM: A Unified Policy and Method-Agnostic Benchmark for Humanoid Single-Leg Balance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00500)
*Yikai Zhou,Xingyun Wang,Jieming Cui,Bozhou Chen,Yikai Fan,Yixin Zhu,Wenxin Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了可部署的动态质心（Dynamic-CoM）观测，解决了人形机器人单腿平衡问题。现有通用策略在90个测试动作中无一能保持单腿站立，只能通过迈步或跳跃来恢复平衡。作者通过将质心速度相对支撑脚表达，使该观测仅需编码器和IMU即可重建，无需机载线速度传感器。结合从人类姿势控制翻译的奖励库，训练出的FDDC策略在86/90个动作上保持干净的单腿平衡，并成功迁移到真实Unitree G1机器人上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形机器人通用策略虽然能处理敏捷的全身运动，但在最简单的单腿平衡任务上表现糟糕——8个SOTA通用策略在90个测试动作中全部无法保持干净的单腿站立。根本原因是缺乏捕获点（xCoM）这一关键观测，而该观测因需要线速度传感器而从未被用于硬件策略。作者希望将"预防失衡"而非"事后恢复"作为平衡的基本原则。

Method: 1) 提出动态质心观测：将xCoM相对支撑脚表达，使线速度精确抵消，仅用编码器和IMU即可重建，首次实现硬件可部署；2) 构建从人类姿势控制逐项翻译的奖励库，遵循"预防优于修复"的原则；3) 使用非对称FastSAC训练，配备特权评论家，无需蒸馏；4) 发布首个方法无关、可复现的sim2sim单腿平衡基准，在训练不同的模拟器中评估策略。

Result: FDDC策略在90个留出测试动作中的86个（涵盖9个分层姿态类别）保持干净的单腿平衡，并成功迁移到真实Unitree G1机器人上。消融实验表明动态质心观测是最大的单一贡献因素：单独移除它会导致干净单腿平衡得分下降40个百分点。

Conclusion: 作者首次实现了可部署的动态质心观测，将单腿平衡从"每次任务的小技巧"转变为可被整个领域测量和评估的能力。通过开源完整栈和首个可复现的sim2sim基准，为平衡能力建立了标准化的度量框架。

Abstract: Unified humanoid policies handle agile whole-body motion, yet stumble on a simple demand: staying balanced on one leg. On our single-leg-balance benchmark, eight released state-of-the-art general policies hold a clean single-leg stance on 0 of 90 test motions; they stay up only by stepping or hopping, recovering from imbalance rather than preventing it. Prevention needs the capture point (xCoM), the center of mass (CoM) extrapolated by its velocity, which has never driven a hardware policy because it requires a base linear velocity no on-board sensor provides; expressed relative to the support foot, that velocity cancels exactly, leaving an observation reconstructible from encoders and IMU alone. We put this first deployable dynamic-CoM observation directly into the actor that runs on hardware, and pair it with a reward library translated term by term from human postural control, under one principle: prevention over repair. Trained by asymmetric FastSAC with a privileged critic and no distillation, the resulting policy, FDDC (First Deployable Dynamic-CoM), holds clean single-leg balance on 86 of 90 held-out motions across nine stratified pose classes and transfers to a real Unitree G1; in ablation, the dynamic-CoM observation is the single largest driver: removing it alone costs 40 points of clean single-leg balance. We release the full stack with the first method-agnostic, reproducible sim2sim benchmark for humanoid single-leg balance, scoring each policy in a simulator distinct from its training one, a step toward turning balance from a per-task trick into a capability the field can measure.

</details>


### [90] [SSTG-Nav: Metric-Grounded Spatial-Semantic Topological Graphs for Reusable Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00527)
