*Daojie Peng,Bingtao Wang,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SSTG-Nav，一种可复用的度量-语义记忆系统，通过一次性扫描将家庭环境转化为可操作的目标位置，使服务机器人在长期部署中实现可靠、重复的语义导航。在HM3D-v2数据集上，几何成功上限达99.4%，度量定位将SR/SPL提升至0.920/0.603，Top-3恢复策略使Success@3达0.975。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ObjectNav主要被建模为一次性探索任务，忽略了服务机器人长期部署的核心挑战：识别目标物体并不等同于知道可到达的停靠位置，且单次置信地图错误可能导致任务直接失败。机器人应该在熟悉环境中越用越可靠，而非每次请求都从头搜索。

Method: SSTG-Nav构建一个目标无关的可复用度量-语义记忆系统，包含三个核心机制：(1)将一次性扫描转化为可操作的目标位置；(2)跨视角整合语义证据；(3)保留空间上可区分的恢复待命点。系统采用源感知融合和Top-3恢复策略，并基于ROS2/Nav2实现完整的查询到执行流水线。

Result: 在36个场景的1000个HM3D-v2 episodes上，目标无关拓扑达到99.4%几何成功上限；保持语义响应不变时，度量定位将SR/SPL从0.835/0.560提升至0.920/0.603；源感知融合达0.926/0.586；融合感知的Top-3恢复将Success@1/2/3提升至0.928/0.965/0.975，SPL@3达0.601。通过模型、视场角、密度和损坏对照实验验证了增益来源。

Conclusion: 预探索（pre-exploration）是可靠重复语义导航的一种强大实用范式，SSTG-Nav通过可复用记忆系统显著提升机器人在熟悉环境中的长期部署性能，并提供了完整的工程实现验证。

Abstract: Service robots operating for months in the same homes, offices, and facilities should become more reliable with experience instead of searching familiar space from scratch for every request. Yet ObjectNav is predominantly formulated as one-shot exploration, leaving a central deployment challenge unresolved: recognizing an object does not identify a reachable place to stop, and one confident map error can terminate the task. We introduce SSTG-Nav, a reusable metric-semantic memory that turns a one-time survey into actionable object goals, consolidates evidence across viewpoints, and retains spatially distinct recovery standoffs. On 1,000 HM3D-v2 episodes across 36 scenes, our goal-independent topology achieves a 99.4% geometric success ceiling. Holding semantic responses fixed, metric grounding raises SR/SPL from 0.835/0.560 to 0.920/0.603, and source-aware fusion reaches 0.926/0.586. Fusion-aware Top-3 recovery raises Success@1/2/3 to 0.928/0.965/0.975 and reaches 0.601 SPL@3. Model, field-of-view, density, and corruption controls identify where these gains originate, and a ROS2/Nav2 realization demonstrates the complete reusable query-to-execution pipeline. Together, the results establish pre-exploration as a powerful practical regime for dependable, repeated semantic navigation.

</details>


### [91] [Disentangling Visuo-Tactile Foresight: Oracle-Guided Interface Discovery for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00547)
*Zihang Yao,Chaoyue Ding,Yingying Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Oracle Visuo-Tactile Foresight (OVTF)框架，通过解耦世界模型与动作专家，独立研究"未来表征如何有效指导接触丰富的操作"。在该框架下提出非对称相位对齐未来记忆(AFM)，在七个仿真任务上平均成功率32.0%，优于模态隔离基线(23.7%)和UniVTAC-ACT(14.9%)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操作依赖视觉上难以观测的物理交互线索，触觉-视觉世界动作模型虽有前景，但未来信息应如何结构化以被动作专家有效利用这一问题尚未被充分探索。端到端学习难以独立研究该问题，因为行为效果被物理无效的视觉未来、不可靠预测、跨模态不一致的触觉预测以及难以解读的未来到动作接口所混淆。

Method: 提出OVTF受控框架，从仿真中成功轨迹提供配对的RGB和触觉未来，隔离并独立研究"未来到动作"接口。提出非对称相位对齐未来记忆(AFM)：视觉记忆读取未来视觉；每个触觉记忆联合关注自身触觉流与相位对齐的未来视觉，阻断跨触觉访问。与模态隔离未来记忆(IFM)进行对照比较。

Result: 在UniVTAC仿真基准的七个任务上，AFM平均成功率为32.0%，显著优于IFM的23.7%和UniVTAC-ACT的14.9%。

Conclusion: 选择性的相位对齐视觉-触觉路由比完全模态隔离提供了更可操作的未来到动作桥接，验证了触觉记忆引入对齐的未来视觉信息（而非孤立处理）能更有效提升动作专家的表现。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation remains challenging because successful control depends on physical interaction cues that are often weakly observable from vision alone. Recent tactile world action models jointly model future visual observations and tactile signals to guide action generation, but how such futures should be structured for effective use by the action expert remains underexplored. Directly studying this question with learned world action models is difficult because end-to-end behavior entangles physically invalid visual futures, unreliable predictions, inaccurate or cross-modally inconsistent tactile forecasts, and an unreadable future-to-action interface. To make this interface independently studyable, we introduce Oracle Visuo-Tactile Foresight (OVTF), a controlled framework that supplies paired RGB and tactile futures from successful trajectories verified in simulation. By fixing the future provider, OVTF isolates the interface and asks a cleaner question: if the future is successful and physically executable, what representation allows the action expert to absorb its benefit? Within OVTF, we propose Asymmetric Phase-Local Future Memory (AFM), in which visual memory reads future vision, each tactile memory jointly attends to its own tactile stream and phase-aligned future vision, and cross-tactile access is blocked. We compare AFM with Modality-Isolated Future Memory (IFM), which removes visual-to-tactile access and processes each future modality independently. Across seven tasks on the UniVTAC simulation benchmark, AFM achieves 32.0% average success, compared with 23.7% for IFM and 14.9% for UniVTAC-ACT. This controlled comparison shows that selective phase-aligned visual-tactile routing provides a more actionable future-to-action bridge than complete modality isolation.

</details>


### [92] [DexMani: Human-Derived Manipulability Guidance for Dexterous Rotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00554)
*Xiaoyang Chen,Shengcheng Luo,Haoran Guo,Jiaming Jiang,Wanlin Li,Ziyuan Jiao,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexMani是一个将人类示范转化为接触条件可操作演化先验的框架，用于指导强化学习实现灵巧手物体旋转技能，在多种机器人手上均达到最高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧物体旋转是一个序列接触问题，每次支撑、释放和再接触决策既要产生期望的物体运动，又要为后续连续旋转准备好手部构型。现有强化学习方法在特定机器人手上通过试错发现运动模式，但没有显式考虑每个接触转换对手部维持后续旋转能力的影响。

Method: DexMani将人类示范转化为接触条件可操作演化（contact-conditioned manipulability evolution）先验，用于捕获成功的人类接触转换如何重塑手部可用的物体旋转方向。该框架学习这种可操作演化，并利用它引导下游强化学习，从而在具有不同运动学和主动接触配置的机器人上获取旋转技能。

Result: 在Shadow Hand、Allegro Hand和XHand上，DexMani在所有评估场景（包括已见和未见物体）中均达到最高成功率。在LEAP Hand上平均成功率达57.5%，优于其他基线方法，并产生更平滑的旋转运动。

Conclusion: DexMani通过将人类示范中的接触-可操作演化知识迁移到强化学习，有效解决了灵巧旋转中的序列接触规划难题，并在多种异构机器人手上展现了优异的泛化能力和性能。

Abstract: Dexterous object rotation is a sequential contact problem: each support, release, and re-contact decision must both produce the desired object motion, and prepare the hand configuration for continued rotation. Existing reinforcement learning methods discover such movement patterns through trial and error on specific robotic hand embodiments, without explicitly accounting for how each contact transition affects the hand's ability to sustain object rotation in subsequent steps. We introduce DexMani, a framework that transfers human demonstrations as contact-conditioned manipulability evolution. This prior captures how successful human contact transitions reshape the object-rotation directions available to the hand. DexMani then learns this manipulability evolution and uses it to guide downstream reinforcement learning, enabling rotation skills to be acquired across robot embodiments with distinct kinematics and active-contact configurations. Across the Shadow Hand, Allegro Hand, and XHand, DexMani achieves the highest success rates in every evaluated setting for both seen and unseen objects. DexMani reaches an average success rate of 57.5% on LEAP Hand, outperforming other baselines and producing smoother rotatory motions. Project site: https://dexmani.github.io

</details>


### [93] [Latency-Tolerant Cloud-Edge Collaborative Vision-Language-Action Models via Emergent Representational Specialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00569)
*Daojie Peng,Fulong Ma,Bingtao Wang,Sheng Wang,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CloudEdgeVLA，一种云-边缘协同的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略，通过将时间错位建模为表示学习问题，使云模型编码慢变任务特征，边缘轻量头结合最新云特征与本地视觉进行闭环控制，在LIBERO基准上显著优于现有分层和异步方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 十亿参数的VLA策略部署在移动机器人上存在系统冲突：语义推理受益于云端GPU，但闭环控制需要在本地快速响应，且网络延迟和抖动会导致分层/异步策略的慢路径表示过期，现有方法需要显式调度或延迟提示。

Method: 云端的VLA模型将延迟观测编码为缓慢变化的任务特征，边缘轻量头将最新可用的云特征与当前本地视觉结合。训练时，当前帧与随机延迟帧配对相同的当前动作目标，分别走新鲜路径和过期路径，该目标函数促使云表示保留任务级信息，而边缘路径提供状态敏感的校正。

Result: 在四个LIBERO套件中，CloudEdgeVLA在40步均匀延迟窗口下保持63.8-78.0%的成功率，而VLASH最高仅6.4%，评估的单路径基线最高仅3.0%。

Conclusion: 通过从控制环中移除阻塞同步，该设计为可扩展的VLA部署提供了实用路径：云模型可以不断增长，而边缘计算保持轻量且响应迅速。

Abstract: Deploying billion-parameter Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies on mobile robots creates a systems conflict: semantic reasoning benefits from cloud GPUs, whereas closed-loop control must respond locally despite network delay and jitter. Existing hierarchical and asynchronous policies improve throughput, but their slow-path representations can still arrive stale or require explicit scheduling and delay cues. We introduce CloudEdgeVLA, a cloud-edge policy that treats temporal misalignment as a representation-learning problem. A cloud VLA encodes delayed observations into slowly varying task features, while a lightweight edge head combines the latest available cloud feature with current local vision. During training, current and randomly delayed frames are paired with the same current action target in fresh and stale paths. This objective encourages the cloud representation to preserve task-level information while the edge path supplies state-sensitive corrections. Across four LIBERO suites, CloudEdgeVLA retains 63.8--78.0% success with a 40-step uniform-delay window, whereas VLASH reaches at most 6.4% and the evaluated single-path baselines at most 3.0%. By removing blocking synchronization from the control loop, the design offers a practical route to scalable VLA deployment in which cloud models can grow while edge computation remains lightweight and responsive.

</details>


### [94] [Grasp Execution Without a Planner: Configuration-Space Grasp Distance Fields with Certified Safety & Guaranteed Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00600)
*Clinton Enwerem,John S. Baras,Calin Belta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Grasp Distance Fields (GDFs)，一种在机械臂-手部配置空间中定义的光滑软最小距离场，通过静态反馈律沿其负梯度方向执行抓取，无需规划器、存储轨迹或抓取点选择，并通过CBF-CLF QP过滤保证安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多指抓取执行架构需要规划无碰撞轨迹并跟踪，但执行期间物体位姿不确定性或扰动会使规划轨迹失效，被迫进行昂贵的重新规划。作者旨在消除这一重规划步骤，实现无需规划器的鲁棒抓取执行。

Method: 构建Grasp Distance Fields (GDFs)，即对抓取候选配置集合取软最小（softmin）距离场；用静态反馈律沿负梯度执行抓取；通过CBF-CLF二次规划过滤命令，约束自碰撞、工作空间、物体和障碍物间隙；在预抓取配置处用滞回方式切换模式，以物体碰撞规避换取接触接纳；用扳手质量屏障保持力闭合裕度。

Result: 在固定基座机械臂和Unitree G1人形机器人上（均配欠驱动手），在杂乱动态场景中成功安全抓取并举起50个物体中的46个；执行抓取保留合成质量裕度的中位数为94%；QP求解时间0.09ms/20ms控制步；逐步softmax权重确认控制器执行最近候选。

Conclusion: GDF控制器消除了对规划器、存储轨迹和独立抓取选择的需求，在动态杂乱场景中实现了鲁棒且安全的抓取执行，具有计算效率高、质量保持好的优势。

Abstract: Standard multifingered grasp execution architectures plan a collision-free trajectory to a selected grasp pose and track it with a feedback law. Execution-time object pose uncertainty or perturbations may invalidate the planned trajectory, forcing a costly replanning step. We present Grasp Distance Fields (GDFs), a smooth softmin distance field over grasp configurations in the arm-hand configuration space. Our controller executes a grasp by following this field's negative gradient with a stationary feedback law, eliminating the need for a planner, stored trajectory, or grasp selection. For safety, we filter this command through a CBF-CLF quadratic program (QP) constraining self-collision, workspace, object, and obstacle clearance, reporting impeded progress as explicit slack. We prove that the softmin tracks the true set distance within \(\log N/ρ\) for \(N\) candidates and smoothing parameter \(ρ\), and that the filtered loop renders the safe set forward invariant. Since no smooth field captures hand-object contact switching, we switch modes with hysteresis at a pregrasp configuration, trading object collision avoidance for contact admission. A wrench-quality barrier then keeps the realized grasp's force-closure margin within a prescribed tolerance of its value at hold onset. We evaluate in kinematic simulation on a fixed-base arm and a Unitree G1 humanoid, both fitted with the same underactuated hand. Our controller navigates cluttered and dynamic scenes to safely reach for, grasp, and lift 46 of 50 objects spanning primitive, household, and adversarial classes. Across those lifts, the executed grasps retain a median 94\% of their synthesized quality margin, at 0.09 ms QP solve time per 20 ms control step. Per-step softmax weights confirm that our controller executes the nearest candidate, obviating a separate selection step. Project page: www.clintonenwerem.com/gdf.

</details>


### [95] [From Failures to Supervision: DynamicEnvPlan for Robust Long-Horizon Embodied Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00613)
*Hao Yuan,Yuxin Wang,Lei Ji,Zhiwei Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DynamicEnvPlan，一个面向动态物理世界的闭环高层规划框架，通过规划、扰动和受控修正模块将动态执行状态转化为恢复导向的训练轨迹，采用分阶段监督微调训练规划器，在104种任务场景组合下将成功率从33.3%提升至76.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理世界交互本质上是动态的，环境在执行过程中可能发生变化，需要代理在非平稳条件下调整计划。现有具身任务基准虽然能暴露此类失败，但通常仅将失败视为评估结果，而非用于训练代理恢复的可学习信号。

Method: DynamicEnvPlan采用闭环框架，包含：1) 数据合成设计，由规划、扰动和受控修正三个模块组成，将动态执行状态转化为恢复导向的轨迹；2) 分阶段监督微调，使规划器同时从名义执行和扰动恢复轨迹中学习；3) 集成人形代理、高层原始技能、结构化语义记忆和可控扰动。

Result: 在104个任务场景组合（涵盖i.i.d.、组合泛化和分布外设置）上，DynamicEnvPlan将基础规划器的成功率从33.3%提升至76.2%，并在包括安全性和可负担性合规在内的全部七项评估指标上均有改善。

Conclusion: 通过将环境扰动和执行的动态失败转化为可学习的恢复信号，动态环境中的长时程具身规划能力可以得到显著提升，这为物理世界交互中的非平稳适应提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Physical-world interaction is inherently dynamic, as environments can evolve during execution, requiring agents to adapt their plans under non-stationary conditions. We study this challenge through long-horizon embodied planning under environment deviations and execution uncertainty. Existing embodied-task benchmarks can expose such failures, but these failures are usually treated as evaluation outcomes instead of learnable signals for training agents to recover. In this work, we introduce DynamicEnvPlan, a closed-loop framework for high-level planning in dynamic environments. It extends embodied task execution with humanoid agents, high-level primitive skills, structured semantic memory, and controllable perturbations. Our data synthesis design consists of planning, perturbation, and guarded correction modules that turn dynamic execution states into recovery-oriented traces. The resulting traces are used for staged supervised fine-tuning, enabling the planner to learn from both nominal execution and perturbed recovery trajectories. Using 104 task-scene combinations spanning i.i.d., compositional generalization, and out-of-distribution settings for fine-tuning and evaluation, DynamicEnvPlan boosts success rate from 33.3% for the base planner to 76.2%, while improving across all seven evaluation metrics critical to physical-world interaction, including safety and affordance compliance.

</details>


### [96] [Learning-Based Motion Planning for Dynamic Environments: From Foundational Algorithms to Emerging Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00625)
*Zongyuan Shen,Shalabh Gupta,Shancheng Zhao,Dehua Zhou,Gao Wang,Rui Cheng,Yaming Ou,Zhongqiang Ren,Yikui Zhai,C. L. Philip Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了2015至2025年间动态环境下运动规划的研究进展，重点探讨基于学习的方法如何扩展、补充或与传统经典规划方法相互作用，提出了一种"学习角色"分类法来系统梳理现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境中的运动规划是机器人学的核心问题，近年来基于学习的方法大量涌现，但缺乏一个系统的框架来理解这些学习方法与经典规划方法之间的关系，以及它们在规划流程中所扮演的不同角色。

Method: 提出"学习角色"分类法，将现有方法分为四类：直接策略学习、学习增强的经典规划、混合规划和训练增强方法。对每类方法总结了问题设定、代表性算法、关键思想、集成机制、优点和局限性，并分析了观测表示、预测不确定性、交互建模、规划器集成、安全约束和训练策略等关键因素。

Result: 系统梳理了2015至2025年间动态环境运动规划的代表性工作，通过分类学框架揭示了学习方法与经典规划方法之间的互补与交互关系，并识别了塑造学习型运动规划的关键技术因素。

Conclusion: 指出了未来研究方向，包括模拟到现实的迁移差距、安全可认证规划、密集人群导航、感知-规划耦合以及具身人工智能等开放挑战。

Abstract: Motion planning in dynamic environments is a fundamental problem in robotics, aiming to generate safe and efficient paths, trajectories, or control actions in the presence of moving obstacles, uncertain predictions, and multi-agent interactions. It has broad applications in autonomous driving, service robotics, warehouse logistics, human-robot collaboration, crowd navigation, and multi-robot systems. This survey reviews representative works published primarily between 2015 and 2025, with a particular focus on how recent learning-based advances extend, complement, or interact with classical planning foundations. We first revisit classical planning methods as algorithmic foundations and reference frameworks for learning-based extensions. We then propose a role-of-learning taxonomy that categorizes existing methods according to how learning participates in the planning pipeline, including direct policy learning, learning-augmented classical planning, hybrid planning, and training enhancement methods. For each category, we summarize the main problem settings, representative algorithms, key ideas, integration mechanisms, strengths, and limitations. We further analyze how observation representations, prediction uncertainty, interaction modeling, planner integration, safety constraints, and training strategies shape learning-based motion planning in dynamic environments. Finally, we discuss open challenges and future directions, including sim-to-real gap, safe and certifiable planning, dense crowd navigation, perception-planning coupling, and embodied AI.

</details>


### [97] [FlowPilot: Real-Time World-Action Modeling for Agile UAV Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00635)
*Runqing Wang,Ding Yu,Pengyuan Min,Xinhong Zhang,Wei Xiao,Yu Hu,Jie Chen,Fu Zhang,Gang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlowPilot是一个紧凑的世界-动作联合模型，通过流匹配同时去噪未来深度观测和可执行轨迹，实现实时机载无人机导航，在Jetson Orin NX上推理时间低于18ms，物理四旋翼飞行器在杂乱室内和森林环境中达到5.5m/s速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两类局限：map-then-optimize管线需要局部重建，计算开销大；端到端策略缺乏显式场景预测能力。作者希望设计一个既能进行场景预测又能输出可执行轨迹的紧凑模型，实现实时机载导航。

Method: FlowPilot采用双流混合Transformer结构，通过共享注意力耦合视频专家和动作专家，利用流匹配联合去噪未来深度观测和可执行轨迹。动作参数化为7阶Bernstein多项式（当前状态约束初始控制点，网络预测5个自由控制点），产生C^2连续参考轨迹并具有闭式速度、加速度和加加速度。训练采用三级别深度金字塔（高吞吐仿真、照片真实感仿真、真实机载数据）。部署时以动作为中心，仅输出轨迹。

Result: 在闭环仿真中，FlowPilot在增加杂波和命令速度高达8m/s的条件下优于学习和优化基线。在物理四旋翼上，完整感知到动作管线在Jetson Orin NX上运行时间低于18ms，在杂乱室内和森林环境中仅使用机载传感和计算达到5.5m/s速度。

Conclusion: FlowPilot通过将未来场景预测与轨迹生成联合建模，在紧凑模型规模下同时实现了精确的轨迹生成能力和高效的真实世界部署性能，验证了世界-动作联合建模在无人机导航中的有效性。

Abstract: We present FlowPilot, a compact world-action model for real-time onboard UAV navigation from depth. Unlike map-then-optimize pipelines that require local reconstruction or end-to-end policies that lack explicit scene prediction, FlowPilot jointly denoises future depth observations and executable trajectories with flow matching. A dual-stream mixture-of-transformers couples video and action experts through shared attention, allowing future-scene prediction and trajectory generation to inform each other. At deployment, the model runs action-centrically and outputs only a trajectory. To ensure trackability, actions are parameterized as degree-7 Bernstein polynomials: the current state constrains the initial control points, and the network predicts five free control points, yielding C^2-continuous references with closed-form velocity, acceleration and jerk. FlowPilot is trained on a three-level depth pyramid spanning high-throughput simulation, photorealistic simulation, and real onboard data. In closed-loop simulation, it outperforms learning- and optimization-based baselines under increasing clutter and commanded speeds up to 8m/s. On a physical quadrotor, the full perception-to-action pipeline runs in under 18ms on a Jetson Orin NX and reaches 5.5m/s in cluttered indoor and forest environments using only onboard sensing and computation.

</details>


### [98] [Assistant Placement Aria: A Benchmark for Egocentric Placement Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00652)
*Amir Belder,Gonçalo Dias Pais,Refael Vivanti,Omri Carmi,Daniel DeTone,Oren Shrout,Ido Gattegno,Ayellet Tal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个面向虚拟放置（VP）任务的基准数据集Assistant Placement Aria，涵盖合成和真实室内场景，包含2D面板放置、坐姿建议和电视放置三个子任务，并搭配2D图像、3D点云和文本描述多模态数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人放置任务通常只关注单一预定义目标位置，而虚拟放置（VP）需要识别场景中所有符合人类意图和偏好的合理目标位置，涉及全局与局部推理，但目前缺乏相关基准数据集来推动这一领域的研究。

Method: 构建了包含合成与真实室内场景的基准数据集，围绕VP问题设计了三个任务（2D面板放置、坐姿建议、电视放置），每个场景提供2D图像、3D点云和场景内物体的文本描述，并在该基准上评估了多种基础模型的目标检测和分割性能。

Result: 提供了一个多模态、多任务的VP基准数据集，并完成了对现有基础模型在该基准上的初步评估，揭示了当前模型在VP任务上的表现与不足之处。

Conclusion: 通过贡献该基准，旨在鼓励更多研究投入虚拟放置这一具有挑战性且依赖数据的领域，推动机器人在放置任务中更好地理解人类意图和场景语义。

Abstract: Human assistance in robotics spans around several tasks such as navigation, object manipulation, and placement, where a key challenge is selecting target destinations that align with human intentions or preferences. We focus on this challenge in the context of Virtual Placement (VP), the task of identifying all plausible target locations given scene context and human-centric constraints. This differs from traditional placement tasks that typically focus on a single, predefined target location. The VP problem is complex, as it requires both global and local reasoning about the scene's geometry, semantics, and plausibility. To address this gap, we introduce {\bf Assistant Placement Aria}, the first benchmark to explore diverse aspects of VP, including global, local, and human-centric constraints. It contains both synthetic and real indoor scenes annotated for three tasks: (i)~2D Panel Placement, (ii)~Sitting Suggestion, and (iii)~TV Placement. Each scene includes 2D images, a 3D point cloud, and a textual description of the objects within the scene. By contributing this benchmark, we aim to encourage further research in this underexplored and challenging field that is critically dependent on relevant data. We also evaluate several foundation models for object detection and segmentation on our benchmark.

</details>


### [99] [Staged Multi-Agent Training (SMAT) for Hip Exoskeletons: Metabolic and Biomechanical Validation of a Simulation-Trained Co-Adaptive Controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00715)
*Yifei Yuan,Jakob Wolf,Ghaith Androwis,Xianlian Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次对SMAT（分段多智能体训练）策略进行生理学验证，在髋部外骨骼上对8名健康成年人测试，发现主动辅助相比被动设备降低净代谢率19.7%（p<0.001），且策略在未经个体重训练的情况下对行走速度和地形具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的控制器可以在纯物理仿真中训练外骨骼辅助策略，但很少有人-设备共同适应（co-adaptation）的控制器通过整机代谢测量在真实用户上得到验证。共同适应具有挑战性：设备改变关节动力学后，穿戴者会重组神经肌肉协调，形成非平稳学习问题。因此需要一种能应对该问题的控制器，并需在真实用户上进行生理学验证。

Method: 采用SMAT四阶段课程训练方法，逐步训练肌肉骨骼人体智能体和双侧髋部外骨骼智能体。将训练好的策略部署到髋部外骨骼上，对8名健康成年人进行测试，使用间接测热法测量无外骨骼、被动和主动三种条件下的代谢消耗，并进行生物力学分析和跨速度、跨地形的泛化测试。

Result: 主动辅助相比被动设备将净代谢率降低19.7%（p<0.001）；生物力学分析显示所有受试者的髋部机械功主要为正向（正功率比0.98）；策略能够泛化到不同行走速度和地形条件。

Conclusion: 单个纯仿真训练的SMAT策略，无需个体特定重训练，即可在真实用户上带来显著的代谢获益，并在训练条件之外保持鲁棒性，为基于仿真训练的外骨骼控制提供了首个生理学层面的验证证据。

Abstract: Learning-based controllers can deliver exoskeleton assistance after training entirely in physics-based simulation, yet few controllers that address human-device co-adaptation have been validated on real users by whole-body metabolic measurement, the standard benchmark for assistive walking. Co-adaptation is challenging: as the device alters joint dynamics, the wearer reorganizes neuromuscular coordination, producing a non-stationary learning problem. Staged Multi-Agent Training (SMAT), a four-stage curriculum that progressively trains a musculoskeletal human actor and a bilateral hip exoskeleton actor, was introduced and shown to reduce simulated hip-muscle activation and provide positive assistance on hardware. This article provides the first physiological validation of SMAT. The policy was deployed on a hip exoskeleton and tested with eight healthy adults, with metabolic cost measured by indirect calorimetry across no-exoskeleton, passive, and active conditions. Active assistance lowered net metabolic rate by 19.7% relative to the passive device (p < 0.001). Biomechanical analysis confirmed predominantly positive hip mechanical power across all subjects (positive-power ratio 0.98), and the policy generalized across walking speeds and terrains. Together, these results show that a single simulation-trained SMAT policy, deployed without subject-specific retraining, delivers a significant metabolic benefit on real users while remaining robust beyond the conditions it was trained on.

</details>


### [100] [OmniAI: A Surface-Adaptive Aerial Projection Interface for Human--Drone Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00721)
*Nikita Kuzmin,Yuhua Jin,Georgii Demianchuk,Mariya Lezina,Fawad Mehboob,Ivan Valuev,Nikolai Lutsenko,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OmniAI是一种具身空中智能体，通过机载屏幕与环境表面之间的投影切换实现自适应表面交互，并集成语音和手势控制，为无人机与人类交互提供移动空间增强现实界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在人类环境中缺乏空间锚定的通信界面，需要更具情境感知的交互方式。

Method: 采用伺服驱动的MEMS激光投影仪，将Web增强版LLM生成的文本和图像响应投影到检测到的表面；通过RGB-D感知和RANSAC平面拟合在线检测投影表面（无需预建地图）；支持等价的语音和空中手势控制。

Result: 实现了无人机运动与投影内容的统一控制，演示了结合语音、手势、自适应投影与空中机动性的移动空间AR交互系统。

Conclusion: OmniAI展示了无人机作为移动空间AR界面在情境感知人机交互中的潜力，为未来空中智能体的空间通信提供了新方向。

Abstract: Drones in human environments often lack spatially grounded in- terfaces for situated communication. We present OmniAI, an em- bodied aerial agent that supports surface-adaptive interaction by switching projection between an onboard screen and nearby en- vironmental surfaces. A servo-actuated MEMS laser projector renders text-and-image responses from a web-augmented LLM pipeline. Projection surfaces are detected online using RGB-D sensing and RANSAC plane fitting, without pre-mapped geometry. OmniAI provides functionally equivalent voice and gesture con- trol for both drone motion and projected content. By combining speech, mid-air gestures, adaptive projection, and aerial mobility, OmniAI demonstrates a mobile spatial AR interface for context- aware human-drone interaction.

</details>


### [101] [SelfWAM: A Self-Grounded Unified World Action Model for Fast Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00725)
*Bikang Pan,Fan Liu,Haotao Lu,Jingya Wang,Ye Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SelfWAM 是一个统一的自锚定世界动作模型，基于模态特化的混合Transformer架构，通过联合预测动作、动作条件化的未来RGB帧和机器人自遮挡掩码，将未来预测与机器人可见身体及其动作引发的运动进行锚定，从而提升策略性能并保持快速推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）在条件化未来预测时仅依赖任务提示和观测上下文，无法捕捉动作特定的后果，导致预测的是通用任务进展而非执行动作的具体影响。

Method: 1) 设计基于模态特化混合Transformer（MoT）的统一架构；2) 联合预测动作、动作条件化的未来RGB帧和机器人自遮挡掩码；3) 在联合训练中允许未来视觉查询关注演示动作的清晰副本，使视频分支成为动作特定的后果模型，同时保持动作独热的快速推理路径；4) 使用提示特定的目标进行未来机器人自遮挡掩码预测，去除外观细节并提供与条件动作紧密耦合的时序目标。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0和真实世界操作任务上的实验表明，SelfWAM能够产生更具动作敏感性的未来预测，保持快速的策略推理，并提升策略性能。

Conclusion: 通过清洁动作条件化和未来自遮挡掩码监督，SelfWAM使未来预测更直接地反映执行动作如何改变机器人的可见运动及周围场景，从而提升策略学习效果。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) improve robot policy learning by jointly modeling actions and future observations. However, conditioning future prediction only on the task prompt and observation context risks capturing generic task progression rather than the action-specific consequences of the executed action. We introduce SelfWAM, a unified self-grounded WAM built on a modality-specialized Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) architecture that jointly predicts actions, action-conditioned future RGB frames, and robot self-masks, thereby grounding future prediction in the robot's visible body and its action-induced motion. During joint training, SelfWAM allows future visual queries to attend to a clean copy of the demonstrated action, turning the video branch into an action-specific consequence model while leaving the fast action-only inference path unchanged. To focus video learning on action-relevant visual changes, we use prompt-specific objectives for future robot self-mask prediction, which removes appearance details and provides a target whose temporal evolution is tightly coupled with the conditioning action. Together, clean-action conditioning and future self-mask supervision make future predictions more directly reflect how the executed action changes the robot's visible motion and the surrounding scene. Experiments on RoboTwin 2.0 and real-world manipulation tasks show that SelfWAM produces more action-sensitive futures and preserves fast policy inference, while improving policy performance.

</details>


### [102] [Push-Wiper: Toward General-Purpose Robotic Cleaning across Varied Stains and Surfaces with Segmented Pushing Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00730)
*Renhao Lu,Mingxin Wang,Chenyang Cao,Yang Yang,Guoping Pan,Kangkang Dong,Yi Cheng,Houde Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Push-Wiper框架，将高粘度污渍清洁重构为聚合问题，通过分段推挤轨迹聚集污渍，结合扩散策略生成自适应动作序列，清洁分数比基线方法高130%，并能零样本泛化到固体残留、液体溢出、曲率曲面等场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高粘度污渍具有高黏度和复杂流变特性，传统擦拭会扩散污渍，而擦洗虽摩擦力强但可能损伤表面，亟需一种既能有效清洁又不损伤表面的机器人清洁方法。

Method: 将清洁问题重构为聚合问题：使用海绵通过分段推挤轨迹逐步聚集污渍，再通过后处理阶段剥离聚合材料并实现海绵自清洁。采用逐步策略进行污渍聚集，利用Diffusion Policy生成自适应推挤动作序列，通过任意表面姿态插值器（ASPI）和混合力-位置控制器执行。

Result: 清洁分数（污渍去除面积百分比）比基线方法最高提高130%，且无需额外训练即可零样本迁移到固体残留、液体溢洒、未见过的粘性污渍以及不同几何形状的曲面。

Conclusion: Push-Wiper在清洁效果和泛化能力上均表现优异，为高粘度污渍的机器人表面清洁提供了一种有效且可推广的解决方案。

Abstract: Viscous stains, characterized by high viscosity and complex rheological properties, remain a major challenge for robotic surface cleaning. Conventional wiping often spreads the stain, while scrubbing provides stronger friction but risks damaging the surface. In this paper, we propose Push-Wiper, a framework that reformulates viscous stain cleaning as an aggregation problem. Push-Wiper employs a sponge to progressively gather stains through segmented pushing trajectories, followed by a post-processing phase that detaches the aggregated material and enables sponge self-cleaning. We adopt a stepwise strategy for stain gathering and leverage Diffusion Policy to generate adaptive pushing action sequences. These sequences are executed through our Arbitrary Surface Pose Interpolator (ASPI) and a hybrid force-position controller, allowing the method to generalize to stains with diverse spatial distributions. Push-Wiper achieves a cleaning score (CS), defined as the percentage of stain area removed, up to 130% higher than baseline methods. Without additional training, Push-Wiper also transfers in a zero-shot manner to solid residues, liquid spills, unseen viscous stains, and curved surfaces with varying geometries. Our experiments demonstrate the cleaning effectiveness of Push-Wiper and its strong generalization ability. The project website is available at https://push-wiper.github.io/.

</details>


### [103] [When Prompts Control Robots: Prompt Injection Attacks in Multi-Agent Robotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00747)
*Neha Nagaraja,Amisha Bagari,Hayretdin Bahsi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地研究了LLM多智能体机器人系统中的提示注入攻击，涵盖了直接注入和间接注入两种方式，发现在单智能体和多智能体场景下攻击均能诱导对抗行为并降低任务完成率，且攻击可通过共享提示结构在智能体间传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型被越来越多地集成到自主机器人系统中用于任务规划和控制，但这种集成使其面临提示注入攻击的风险，可能导致不安全决策和物理伤害。多智能体设置通过跨智能体污染和更广泛的攻击面进一步增加了风险，但此前缺乏对多智能体LLM机器人系统中提示注入攻击的系统性研究。

Method: 作者在基于LLM的多智能体机器人系统中评估提示注入攻击，实验设计包含：（1）直接注入（针对任务指令）和间接注入（通过感知模块）两种攻击途径；（2）在单智能体和多智能体两种设置下进行实验；（3）变化攻击目标复杂度与注入策略；（4）分析共享提示结构导致的攻击传播机制；（5）考察架构变化对LLM查询及攻击成功率的影响。

Result: 实验表明提示注入可以诱导对抗行为并降低任务完成率。攻击可以通过共享提示结构从一个智能体传播到其他智能体，其影响程度取决于提示组成方式和被攻击的目标智能体。此外，架构变化会影响LLM查询方式，进而影响攻击成功率。

Conclusion: 这是首个系统性地研究多智能体LLM机器人系统中提示注入攻击的工作，揭示了该安全威胁的严重性和传播机制，为后续防御研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly integrated into autonomous robotic systems for task planning and control, but this integration exposes them to prompt injection attacks that can lead to unsafe decisions and physical harm. Multi-agent settings increase the risks through cross-agent contamination and broader attack surfaces. In this paper, we evaluate prompt injection attacks against an LLM-based multi-agent robotic system, considering both direct injections into task instructions and indirect injections through perception modules. In our experiments across varying attack-goal complexities and injection strategies in both single-agent and multi-agent settings, we show that prompt injection can induce adversarial actions while reducing task completion. We find that attacks can propagate from one agent to others through shared prompt structures, with impacts varying depending on prompt composition and the targeted agent. We further analyze how architectural changes affect LLM queries and, consequently, the attack success. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study that systematically investigates prompt injection attacks in a multi-agent LLM-based robotic system.

</details>


### [104] [ORCESTRA: VLM-driven Visual Robot programming in Mixed Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00775)
*Ivan Snegirev,Elizaveta Semenyakina,Mikhail Konenkov,Artem Lykov,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ORCESTRA是一个混合现实（MR）系统，通过无代码路径点示教和语言引导控制来编程机器人数字孪生。系统支持用户在透视MR工作空间中放置机器人孪生、示教轨迹、保存机器人相对坐标系下的示教片段，或通过语音/文字指令由视觉-语言模型转换为结构化的数字孪生计划。两种交互模式共享底层后端，包含度量接地、具身感知验证、预览、确认和数字孪生执行模块。系统支持多种异构机器人形态（固定基座机械臂、移动底座和人形机器人），证明MR验证可作为语言引导机器人编程在物理部署前的安全层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统语言引导的机器人编程缺乏有效的安全验证机制，直接在物理机器人上执行存在风险。作者希望通过MR数字孪生技术，在部署前提供安全验证层，同时支持无代码示教和语言控制两种模式，降低机器人编程门槛并提升安全性。

Method: 构建ORCESTRA混合现实系统，支持两种交互模式：(1) 无代码路径点示教，用户可在MR空间中放置机器人孪生并直接示教轨迹；(2) 语言引导控制，通过视觉-语言模型将自然语言指令转换为结构化的数字孪生计划。两种模式共享后端管线，包括度量接地、具身感知验证、预览、确认及数字孪生执行。

Result: 系统成功支持了多种异构机器人形态（固定基座机械臂、移动底座、人形机器人），验证了MR环境中的数字孪生验证可作为语言引导机器人编程的安全层，在物理部署前有效进行方案验证。

Conclusion: ORCESTRA证明了混合现实数字孪生系统能够将无代码示教与语言引导控制统一到一个框架中，为异构机器人提供可扩展的、兼具易用性与安全性的编程方案，MR验证是物理部署前的重要安全保障手段。

Abstract: ORCESTRA is a mixed-reality system for programming robot digital twins through no-code waypoint teaching and language-guided control. In a passthrough mixed-reality workspace, users place robot twins on real surfaces, teach trajectories, save robot-relative episodes, or issue spoken/typed commands that a vision-language model converts into structured digital-twin plans. Both interaction modes share a backend for metric grounding, embodiment-aware validation, preview, confirmation, and digital-twin execution. The system supports heterogeneous robot embodiments, including fixed-base manipulators, a mobile base, and a humanoid robot, demonstrating MR validation as a safety layer for language-guided robot programming before physical deployment.

</details>


### [105] [SIPTraj: Map-Free End-to-End Trajectory Prediction via Physics-Guided Scene Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00779)
*Feifei Liu,Zejun Wei,Haozhe Wang,Yazhi Ye,Yuying Zhang,Jintao Cheng,Chi Man Vong,Xieyuanli Chen,Xiaoyu Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Trajectory prediction of surrounding agents is a prerequisite for safe planning and decision making in autonomous driving. Without high-definition (HD) maps, sensor-derived bird's-eye-view (BEV) features provide no explicit lane topology or drivable-area priors, making it inherently difficult to ground each agent in its surrounding scene context. Moreover, physical feasibility remains difficult to capture through data-driven learning alone, as kinematic constraints on agent motion cannot be explicitly encoded without structured supervision. Existing map-free predictors extract scene context in an agent-agnostic manner through a single fusion step and treat physical constraints only as output-level penalties, leaving both challenges unaddressed. We propose SIPTraj, a map-free trajectory prediction framework that jointly addresses scene grounding and physical feasibility. SIPTraj introduces a Hierarchical Agent-Scene Encoder (HASE) progressively grounding each agent in agent-guided scene evidence and refining inter-agent relations within the scene-grounded space. To tackle physical infeasibility in predicted trajectories, we develop a Physics-Guided Iterative Decoder (PGID). It conditions decoding on instantaneous kinematic states, propagating physical supervision into internal representations rather than output trajectories alone. Extensive experiments on nuScenes and Argoverse 2 Sensor show that SIPTraj surpasses prior map-free predictors and strong map-based baselines without any HD map at inference. Our code will be released as open-source.

</details>


### [106] [StochSIPP: Safe Interval Path Planning in Stochastic Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00792)
*Ajith Kemisetti,Shahaf S. Shperberg,Yoonchang Sung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出StochSIPP，一种在时间依赖的不确定阻塞条件下进行精确应急规划的算法。它通过SIPP生成认证安全的宏动作，并结合有界AND/OR搜索，在局部观测到不确定边/顶点状态时选择行动，保证碰撞安全并最小化期望到达时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在时间依赖的不确定阻塞环境中安全导航需要提前预测观测结果再决策运动，但现有保守固定路径规划器在门控场景中无法给出任何计划，需要一种能够在不确定性下进行条件性规划的精确方法。

Method: StochSIPP采用两个核心组件：(1) 使用SIPP（Safe Interval Path Planning）生成认证安全的宏动作，这些动作终止于下一个观测点或目标；(2) 在缓存的动作-观测图上进行有界AND/OR搜索，为每个可达的观测结果选择最优动作。通过乐观和鲁棒的SIPP松弛提供可采纳的上下界来加速搜索。

Result: 在受控路网实例上的实验表明，StochSIPP在保持安全固定路径基线成功率的同时减少了到达时间，并能求解保守固定路径规划器无法给出计划的门控场景。可扩展性研究显示，随着同时观测的不确定状态数量增加，计算复杂度快速增长。

Conclusion: 当确定性安全间隔真实安全、传感精确且执行遵循计划时间时，所生成的策略可证明无碰撞；在概率正确且动作/结果生成完整的前提下，该策略在路网和时域内最小化期望到达时间。

Abstract: Safe navigation under uncertain time-dependent blockage requires anticipating observations before committing to motion. We present StochSIPP, an exact contingent planner for temporal roadmaps with uncertain edge and vertex statuses revealed locally during execution. StochSIPP uses SIPP to generate certified-safe macro-actions that terminate at the next observation or the goal, and bounded AND/OR search over a cached action--observation graph to select actions for every reachable observation outcome. Optimistic and robust SIPP relaxations provide admissible lower and upper bounds for bounded AND/OR search. When every interval declared deterministically safe is truly safe, sensing is exact, and execution follows the planned timing, the resulting policy is provably collision-free. With correct independent probabilities and complete action and outcome generation, it minimizes expected arrival time within the roadmap and horizon. Experiments on controlled roadmap instances show that StochSIPP preserves the observed success of safe fixed-path baselines while reducing arrival time, and solves gated scenarios in which conservative fixed-path planners return no plan. A scalability study further reveals rapid growth as the number of simultaneously observed uncertain statuses increases.

</details>


### [107] [DynamicWAM: Dual-Path Motion Conditioning for World-Action Models in Dynamic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00793)
*Yunfan Lou,Hewen Gao,Xiyu Zhu,Zhuoran Qiao,Xuan Han,Yifan Yang,Yifan Ye,Boxian Yao,Zhibo Pang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DynamicWAM是一种面向动态物体操作任务的紧凑型世界-动作模型，通过双路径运动条件注入（历史光流帧的条件编码和运动学描述符注入）来增强机器人的运动感知能力，并采用蒸馏压缩骨干网络和基于实时分块的异步执行机制实现响应式控制，在DOMINO基准和12项真实世界任务中均显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型（WAMs）仅基于当前帧进行条件建模，且使用大型骨干网络同步执行，导致在动态场景中对目标运动的感知能力不足、控制响应迟缓，难以满足动态操作任务的需求。

Method: DynamicWAM提出双路径运动条件建模方法：(1) 历史流条件路径——利用冻结的预训练视频VAE对时间对齐的光流帧与当前观测进行联合编码，保留空间运动结构；(2) 运动学描述符路径——将位移、持续时间、速度、加速度等运动学特征注入动作专家网络，提供运动幅度与时间信息。两条路径通过联合世界-动作注意力进行融合，并采用蒸馏的紧凑骨干网络和基于实时分块（RTC）的异步执行机制实现响应式控制。

Result: 在DOMINO基准上，DynamicWAM达到38.2%的成功率和53.2的操作分数，超越所有评估的基线方法；在涵盖直线、圆形和复合目标运动的12项真实世界任务中，平均成功率达46.7%，比最强基线高出22.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，双路径运动条件建模有效提升了动态物体操作中机器人的运动感知能力与响应控制性能，验证了利用历史运动信息与运动学先验增强世界-动作模型的可行性，为动态场景下的机器人操控提供了新的技术路线。

Abstract: Dynamic manipulation requires robots to infer target motion and respond promptly, yet existing World-Action Models (WAMs) typically condition only on the current frame and execute large backbones synchronously, limiting motion awareness and responsive control in dynamic scenes. We propose DynamicWAM, a compact WAM for dynamic object manipulation with dual-path motion conditioning. DynamicWAM introduces history-flow conditioning, encoding temporally aligned optical-flow frames alongside the current observation through a frozen pretrained video VAE to preserve spatial motion structure, while injecting kinematic descriptors of displacement, duration, velocity, and acceleration into the action expert to provide motion magnitude and timing. The two complementary paths are fused through joint world-action attention. A distilled compact backbone and real-time chunking (RTC)-based asynchronous execution further enable responsive control. On DOMINO, DynamicWAM achieves a 38.2% success rate and a 53.2 manipulation score, outperforming all evaluated baselines. Across 12 real-world tasks spanning linear, circular, and compound target motion, it achieves a 46.7% average success rate, exceeding the strongest baseline by 22.9 percentage points.

</details>


### [108] [LooperMuscle: Fast and Stable Learning of Humanoid Whole-Body Tracking via Structured Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00820)
*Boyi Liu,Qijin Li,Tianqi Yu,Qinrui Yan,Xingxing Zuo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LooperMuscle是一个组合专家策略学习框架，旨在弥合FastSAC方法在全身追踪任务中相比PPO的性能差距，同时保持高训练效率。它结合了语义结构的混合专家actor、专家感知的分布评论家和贡献路由回放与延迟课程调度，形成一个闭环训练系统。实验表明，LooperMuscle在约45分钟内恢复了与PPO差距中的大部分性能，而PPO需要约6小时，FastSAC仅需15分钟但性能欠佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FastSAC类方法显著减少了人形机器人运动训练时间，但在全身追踪任务中相比PPO存在明显的性能退化。作者旨在解决这一速度-性能差距问题，即在保持训练效率的同时恢复追踪质量。

Method: 提出LooperMuscle框架，包含三个关键组件：1) 语义结构的混合专家actor网络；2) 专家感知的分布评论家；3) 贡献路由回放与延迟课程调度。这三个组件形成一个闭环训练循环：专家贡献指导数据路由，路由数据塑造价值学习，价值梯度反过来细化专家专业化。

Result: LooperMuscle在运动追踪精度上大幅优于vanilla FastSAC，同时所需墙钟时间远少于PPO。在FastSAC约15分钟、PPO约6小时的情况下，LooperMuscle仅需约45分钟的仿真训练即可恢复与PPO之间剩余差距的很大一部分。

Conclusion: LooperMuscle为快速策略迭代提供了实用的效率保障，在加速训练的同时有效恢复了追踪性能，填补了FastSAC与PPO之间的速度-性能鸿沟。

Abstract: FastSAC-style methods significantly reduce humanoid motion training time but often suffer from notable performance degradation compared with PPO in whole-body tracking tasks. We target this speed-performance gap by introducing LooperMuscle, a composed expert policy learning framework that restores tracking quality while preserving high training efficiency. LooperMuscle combines a semantically structured mixture-of-experts actor, an expert-aware distributional critic, and contribution-routed replay with deferred curriculum scheduling. These three components form a closed training loop in which expert contributions guide data routing, routed data shape value learning, and value gradients in turn refine expert specialization. Empirically, our approach substantially outperforms vanilla FastSAC in motion tracking accuracy while requiring far less wall-clock time than PPO: where FastSAC trains in about 15 minutes but underperforms, and PPO achieves stronger results but requires about 6 hours, LooperMuscle recovers a substantial fraction of the remaining gap to PPO in roughly 45 minutes of simulation training, delivering practical efficiency for rapid policy iteration. The code will be released to benefit the research community at https://loopermuscle.github.io/.

</details>


### [109] [RIT*: Riemannian Informed Trees for Cost-Adaptive Optimal Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00822)
*Muhayy Ud Din,Ahmed Nadar,Jan Rosell,Irfan Hussain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Riemannian Informed Trees (RIT*)，用黎曼几何原语替代欧几里得原语进行批处理知情搜索规划，并引入碰撞自适应度量精化（CARM）在线学习障碍物邻近代价场。实验显示在高维各向异性环境中性能显著优于BIT*，最高提升63.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于欧几里得度量的规划算法在高维、空间变化度量（各向异性）的环境中性能退化严重，且对先验度量设计依赖性强，需要一种能自适应环境几何结构的规划框架。

Method: RIT*用黎曼度量替换欧几里得原语，构建更紧致的代价一致性知情集合；在各向异性距离度量下进行最近邻搜索；采用级联方案高效评估边代价。同时提出CARM机制，通过碰撞反馈在线学习障碍物邻近代价场，减少对先验度量设计的依赖。

Result: 在2-D到14-D环境中验证：3-D各向异性基准中初始代价中位数较BIT*提升13.0%；6-DOF操作任务中最终代价中位数提升9.0%；14-DOF双臂规划问题中提升24.8-63.5%，而欧几里得知情基线性能退化。

Conclusion: RIT*在低维和空间恒定度量场景下与现有方法竞争，在高维空间变化度量配置空间中能显著产生更低代价的解决方案，性能增益与各向异性和维度成正比。

Abstract: We present Riemannian Informed Trees (RIT*), a planning framework that replaces Euclidean primitives in batch-informed search with their Riemannian counterparts. RIT* constructs a tighter, cost-consistent informed set, performs a nearest-neighbour search under an anisotropic distance metric, and evaluates edge costs efficiently via a cascading scheme. We further introduce a Collision-Adaptive Metric Refinement (CARM), which learns an obstacle-proximity cost field online from collision feedback, reducing the reliance on prior metric design in practical settings. Experiments across environments from 2-D to 14-D show that RIT* is competitive in low-dimensional and spatially constant-metric settings and produces substantially lower-cost solutions when the metric varies spatially in high-dimensional configuration spaces. Performance gains scale with anisotropy and dimension, reaching up to 13.0% improvement in median initial cost over BIT* in the 3-D anisotropic benchmark, up to 9.0% in median final cost over BIT* in 6-DOF manipulation, and 24.8-63.5% in a 14-DOF bimanual planning problem, where Euclidean-informed baselines degrade. Videos and code can be found here: https://muhayyuddin.github.io/ritstar/

</details>


### [110] [GeminiPainter's sequence-formed pipeline comprised of perception, cognition, planning, and action stages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00829)
*Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Aleksey Fedoseev,Iana Zhura,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个自主机器人肖像生成系统，整合实时人脸检测、基于Gemini Vision API的AI素描生成和6自由度协作机械臂绘图，通过感知-认知-行动流水线实现高质量机器人肖像创作，用户评分在素描质量、执行感知和用户体验上均较高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何将计算机视觉、神经艺术抽象、运动优化和机器人控制整合为一个端到端的自主肖像绘制系统，以提升机器人艺术创作的可识别性和用户体验。

Method: 系统采用感知-认知-行动三阶段流水线：首先通过实时人脸检测从视频帧中提取面部区域，随后使用Gemini Vision API将面部转换为极简单线素描，接着通过基于图的路径规划优化笔画顺序，最后由6自由度协作机械臂执行平滑轨迹完成绘制。

Result: 用户5分制评分中，素描质量得分4.33，执行感知得分4.53，用户体验得分4.65，表明生成的机器人肖像具有高识别度、吸引力和参与感。

Conclusion: 该自主机器人肖像生成系统在各维度均获得用户较高评价，验证了融合AI艺术生成与机器人控制的可行性，生成的肖像具有可识别、美观且令人参与的特点。

Abstract: We present an autonomous robotic portrait-generation system combining real-time face detection, AI-based sketch generation, and robotic drawing. The system captures video frames, extracts facial regions, converts them into minimalist single-line sketches using the Gemini Vision API, optimizes stroke order through graph-based path planning, and executes smooth trajectories on a 6-DoF collaborative manipulator. This perception-cognition-action pipeline integrates computer vision, neural artistic abstraction, motion optimization, and robot control. User ratings on a 5-point scale were high for sketch quality 4.33, perceived execution 4.53, and user experience 4.65, indicating recognizable, appealing, and engaging robotic portraits.

</details>


### [111] [Minute-Scale Training for Microrobot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00854)
*Yinghan Sun,Aoji Zhu,Xiang Ji,Yamei Li,Jiachi Zhao,Yun Wang,Li Zhang,Huijun Gao,Lidong Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种学习框架，利用包含10,000多个人工血管环境的全向量化模拟器，结合任务塑形正则化（TSR）奖励框架，使微型机器人导航策略训练时间从数小时/数天缩短至10分钟以内，并支持跨微型机器人类型和导航场景的零样本部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于深度强化学习的微型机器人导航方法学习效率和有效性有限，模型训练需要数小时到数天，严重阻碍了实际部署和参数优化的快速迭代。

Method: 提出包含两个核心组件的学习框架：(1) 构建一个完全向量化的模拟器，包含超过10,000个人工血管环境，并行化动力学模拟、LiDAR启发式感知和可行性检查，实现每秒约190,000个状态转换；(2) 提出任务塑形正则化（TSR）奖励框架，加速收敛、提升最终性能并改善训练效果。

Result: 训练时间缩短至10分钟以内；TSR框架在所有评估场景中将动作变化至少降低33.7%，障碍物间隙至少增加2.1%；支持不同微型机器人类型和导航场景的零样本部署。

Conclusion: 该框架可显著缩短设计循环，加速自主微型机器人的实际部署，为微型机器人导航的快速训练和跨场景泛化提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Microrobots hold significant potential for various applications, where targeted navigation is a basic requirement. Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) has recently emerged as a powerful paradigm for fully autonomous microrobot navigation. Yet, current DRL-based approaches pay limited attention to learning efficiency and effectiveness, requiring hours to days for model training. Consequently, this impedes both rapid practical deployment and parameter optimization. To address these challenges, we present a learning framework that enables effective microrobot navigation policies to be trained within minutes. In the proposed framework, we develop a fully vectorized simulator with more than 10,000 artificial vascular environments, parallelizing dynamics, LiDAR-inspired perception, and feasibility checks across thousands of environments to achieve roughly 190,000 transitions per second. To achieve effectiveness in the fast training, we propose a task-shaping-regularization (TSR) reward framework. The TSR framework accelerates convergence, improves final performance, reduces action variation by at least 33.7%, and increases obstacle clearance by at least 2.1% across all evaluated scenarios. Results show that the proposed learning framework reduces training time to under 10 minutes, while supporting zero-shot deployment across distinct microrobot types and navigation scenarios. Collectively, this framework can substantially shorten the design loop and accelerate the deployment of autonomous microrobots.

</details>


### [112] [Bicycle Acrobatics with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00880)
*Shamel Fahmi,Arianna Ilvonen,Samuel Zapolsky,Yu-Ming Chen,Ravi Boggavarapu,Paul Drews,Ashwin Khadke,Dean Molinaro,Kaiyu Zheng,Alfred Rizzi,Gabriel Nelson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文使用强化学习（RL）使自行车机器人学会并组合多种动态杂技动作，包括跳跃、前空翻、抬前轮等多种技巧，并在仿真和真实硬件上验证了其鲁棒性和长时程任务协调能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自行车机器人虽然快速且节能，但其简单的机械设计、欠驱动和非完整动力学特性使得高度敏捷的动作难以实现，现有方法难以赋予自行车机器人类似腿式机器人的灵活性。

Method: 采用多种强化学习公式（航点跟随、姿态到达、扭转跟踪、引导跟踪和运动模仿）分别训练多种杂技技能策略，并引入一个基于状态触发条件的编排器（orchestrator）在不同策略之间切换，实现长时程的杂技动作协调。

Result: 在定制自行车机器人UMV上验证：可反复越过高达1米的多张桌子、连续15次以上自主跳跃并跟随航点、处理未见过的多桌配置、连续20次以上动作序列、10次以上自主可操控的抬前轮和横跳等动作组合。

Conclusion: 研究表明强化学习可以使自行车机器人达到此前主要与腿式平台相关的敏捷水平，同时保持轮式运动的速度和效率，为自行车杂技奠定了基础。

Abstract: Bicycle robots are fast and energy efficient, but their simple mechanical design and their underactuated and non-holonomic dynamics make highly agile maneuvers difficult to achieve. Here, we use Reinforcement Learning (RL) to enable a bicycle robot to learn and compose a diverse repertoire of dynamic acrobatic stunts. Using different RL formulations such as waypoint following, pose reaching, twist tracking, guided tracking, and motion imitation, the robot acquires autonomous single and multi-table forward and lateral jumps, steerable jumps, front flips, kip-ups, kip-downs, driving, wheelies, bunny hops, and three-point turns. To coordinate these behaviors, we introduce an orchestrator that transitions between policies using state-dependent triggers, enabling robust long-horizon acrobatic stunts. We validate the approach on the Ultra Mobility Vehicle (UMV), a custom bicycle robot, in simulation and hardware. The robot repeatedly traverses tables up to 1 m high, performs more than 15 consecutive autonomous jumps while following waypoints, handles previously unseen multi-table configurations, executes continuous repertoires of kipups, jumps, flips, kip-downs, over more than 20 consecutive trials, and performs more than 10 consecutive autonomous and steerable repertoires of wheelies, lateral jumps, and single-wheel jump downs. These results demonstrate that RL can endow bicycle robots with levels of agility previously associated primarily with legged platforms while preserving the speed and efficiency of wheeled locomotion, establishing a foundation for bicycle acrobatics.

</details>


### [113] [Stipple: Real-Time Incremental Gaussian Splatting with Visual-Inertial Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00931)
*Kilian Northoff,Mateo de Mayo,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将基于Basalt的视觉惯性追踪系统与基于Brush的高效3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）增量式重建方法相结合的新方法，实现了实时同时追踪与重建。通过利用视觉惯性追踪系统生成的信息替代3DGS中繁重的预处理和训练步骤，并采用增量训练策略及多项实用优化，使训练流程能够在追踪线程并行运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）虽然能高效渲染逼真场景，但其繁重的预处理和训练步骤使其不适用于机器人或XR领域需要实时重建的应用。而视觉惯性里程计（VIO）和VI-SLAM系统恰好专为实时应用设计，因此将两者互补整合具有重要价值。

Method: 基于Basalt的视觉惯性追踪系统与基于Brush（Rust编写的GPU厂商无关的高效3DGS实现）的增量式重建方法相结合。将3DGS中繁重的预处理和训练步骤替换为更高效的增量训练策略，该策略可直接利用视觉惯性追踪系统生成的信息；同时提出并整合多项实用改进，提升训练管线效率以支持实时运行，并与追踪线程并行执行。

Result: 实验表明该方法能够实现实时的同时追踪与重建，证明SLAM与3DGS的互补性结合可以产生有前景的实时3D重建结果。

Conclusion: 本文强调了SLAM与3DGS互补融合的价值，展示了利用视觉惯性追踪系统的信息优化3DGS训练流程、实现实时3D重建的可行性，为机器人和XR领域的实时重建应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) provides efficient rendering of photo-realistic scenes, but its heavy preprocessing and training steps make it a poor fit for applications that require real-time reconstruction in robotics or XR. This capability is important since it allows immediate feedback and interaction with new environments. Visual-inertial odometry (VIO) and simultaneous localization and mapping (VI-SLAM) systems, on the other hand, specifically target these real-time applications, which makes them a good choice for integration with 3DGS. We propose a new method that tracks and reconstructs simultaneously in real-time by leveraging an efficient visual-inertial tracking system based on Basalt together with a novel incremental method built on top of Brush, an efficient Rust-based GPU-vendor-agnostic implementation of 3D Gaussian Splatting. We show that many of the heavy preprocessing and training steps of 3DGS can be replaced with a more efficient incremental training strategy that has direct access to the information generated by the visual-inertial tracking system. Furthermore, we propose and combine multiple practical improvements to increase the efficiency of the training pipeline and adapt it to run in real-time, parallel to the tracking thread. This work highlights the value of exploiting the complementary nature of SLAM and 3DGS, and how that can lead to promising results for real-time 3D reconstruction.

</details>


### [114] [VertiAKD: Adaptive Off-Road Kinodynamics on Vertically Challenging Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00945)
*Tong Xu,Chenhui Pan,Francesco Cancelliere,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VertiAKD，一个统一的跨车辆越野运动动力学知识迁移与自适应框架，能够在复杂地形上同时实现车辆配置、轨迹过渡和地形特征的联合编码与知识迁移，无需梯度重训练即可在线优化模型，显著降低预测误差并实现可靠的闭环轨迹跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨车辆自适应方法通常假设平坦地形，而地形感知的运动动力学模型往往需要针对特定平台的数据收集和重新训练，缺乏能够在复杂地形上同时处理多样车辆和连续变化地形的统一框架。

Method: VertiAKD学习一个共享的移动性表征，联合编码车辆配置、轨迹过渡以及局部高程和语义地形特征。当面对新车辆和新地形时，通过函数编码器识别最相关的移动性描述符并迁移其知识来初始化地形感知运动动力学模型，然后利用流式观测进行周期性在线细化，无需基于梯度的重新训练。

Result: 在基于Chrono多物理引擎的Verti-Bench模拟器和Verti-4-Wheeler平台的五种物理配置上评估。仅需一分钟的新轨迹数据和相关地形特征，VertiAKD在长时程预测误差上比直接移动描述符迁移降低最多34.52%，比竞争基线降低94.43%，并在仿真和物理实验中展示了鲁棒的闭环轨迹跟踪能力。

Conclusion: VertiAKD有效验证了地形感知跨车辆知识迁移在精确建模和可靠越野导航中的有效性，为异构车辆编队在复杂地形上的自主移动提供了统一的解决方案。

Abstract: Off-road mobility requires autonomous mobile robots to generalize across heterogeneous vehicle fleets and continuously changing terrain conditions. Existing cross-vehicle adaptation approaches generally assume flat terrain, while terrain-aware kinodynamic models often require platform-specific data collection and retraining. To this end, we propose VertiAKD, a unified framework for transferring and adapting off-road kinodynamic knowledge across diverse vehicles on geometrically and semantically complex terrain simultaneously. VertiAKD learns a shared mobility representation that jointly encodes vehicle configurations, trajectory transitions, and local elevation and semantic terrain features. Given limited data from a novel vehicle operating on unseen terrain, VertiAKD identifies the most relevant mobility descriptors and transfers their knowledge to initialize a terrain-aware kinodynamic model via function encoders, which is then periodically refined online from streaming observations without gradient-based retraining. We evaluate VertiAKD in the Verti-Bench simulator, built on the Chrono multi-physics engine, and on five physical configurations of the Verti-4-Wheeler platform. With only one minute of new trajectory data and associated terrain features, VertiAKD reduces long-horizon prediction error by up to 34.52% over direct mobility descriptor transfer across diverse unseen vehicle configurations and 94.43% over competing baselines. We further demonstrate robust closed-loop trajectory tracking in both simulation and physical experiments, highlighting the effectiveness of terrain-aware cross-vehicle knowledge transfer for accurate modeling and reliable off-road navigation.

</details>


### [115] [GraRe: Grasp Candidate Re-Ranking for Frozen 6-DoF Grasp Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00946)
*Jibao Yuan,Yuhui Zhao,Yinzhen Lv,Chao Xu,Shun Li,Chenxi Deng,Shaofei Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GraRe框架，将6-DoF抓取候选重排序作为独立任务，利用候选属性、壳分层局部几何和物体上下文三种特征经Transformer融合来估计抓取质量，在不改变冻结检测器的情况下显著提升抓取排序性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的6-DoF抓取检测器通常依赖检测器置信度对抓取候选进行排序，但在GraspNet-1Billion上的分析发现，检测器置信度与抓取质量往往严重不对齐，导致高成功率的抓取候选被排到较低位置，影响实际执行效果。

Method: 将抓取候选重排序视为独立于冻结检测器的任务：提取候选属性、壳分层局部几何和物体上下文三种特征，其中候选属性用于条件化局部几何和物体上下文表示，通过Transformer融合三种特征预测抓取质量，最终将预测质量与检测器置信度结合生成最终排序。

Result: 在GraspNet-1Billion数据集上对三种冻结检测器进行实验，均取得一致的性能提升，平均AP最高提升13.60个百分点；真实机器人实验也证明了在杂乱场景中的鲁棒抓取能力。

Conclusion: 改善抓取候选排序为增强冻结的6-DoF抓取检测器提供了一种实用且有效的方法，无需改动检测器本身即可获得显著性能提升。

Abstract: Existing 6-DoF grasp detectors typically rank grasp candidates by detector confidence. However, our analysis on GraspNet-1Billion shows that detector confidence is often poorly aligned with grasp quality, causing successful grasp candidates to be ranked too low during execution. Motivated by this observation, we formulate grasp candidate re-ranking as a separate task for frozen detectors, aiming to improve candidate ordering without changing the detector or its grasp candidates. We propose GraRe, which estimates grasp quality from candidate attributes, shell-stratified local geometry, and object context. Candidate attributes condition the local geometric and object-context representations, and a Transformer fuses all three feature types. The predicted quality is combined with detector confidence to produce the final ranking. Experiments on GraspNet-1Billion with three frozen detectors show consistent improvements, with gains of up to 13.60 points in Average AP. Real-robot experiments further demonstrate robust grasping in cluttered scenes. These results show that improving candidate ranking provides a practical way to enhance frozen 6-DoF grasp detectors.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [Revisiting Classic Thought Experiments to Measure Consciousness for Artificial Intelligence Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00001)
*Peter David Fagan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过守恒一致编码（CCE）框架重新审视莱布尼茨磨坊、图灵测试和塞尔中文房间，提出用任务表现（W_causal,T）与操作意识（κ_T）分别衡量行为成功和内部结构效率，从而将外在表现与支撑表现的内在组织分离开来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典思想实验（莱布尼茨磨坊、图灵测试、塞尔中文房间）长期争论"理解"的本质，但缺乏一个统一的形式化框架来区分"行为表现"与"支撑行为的内在结构"，本文旨在弥合这一分析缺口。

Method: 提出CCE框架，在一个玩具符号设定中形式化两个度量：任务表现（W_causal,T）衡量行为成功，操作意识（κ_T）衡量保留的内部结构支撑行为的效率；通过对比未压缩查找系统与紧凑生成系统的κ_T差异来验证框架。

Result: 未压缩查找系统与紧凑生成系统可以取得相当的行为成功，但在κ_T上差异显著：前者依赖不断扩展且不重复使用的映射存储，后者则复用紧凑内部结构。经典"理解"之争因此可被重新表述为两个维度的分离。

Conclusion: 该框架将关于理解的传统争论重新定义为外在表现与内在组织效率的双维度问题，并指出这种区分对后续AI安全分析具有重要启示意义。

Abstract: This research note revisits Leibniz's mill, Turing's imitation game, and Searle's Chinese Room through the Conservation-Congruent Encoding (CCE) framework. It formalises a toy symbolic setting in which successful behaviour is measured by task performance ($W_{causal,T}$), while the efficiency with which preserved internal structure supports that behaviour is measured by operational consciousness ($κ_T$). Within this setup, an uncompressed lookup system and a compact generative system can in principle achieve comparable behavioural success, yet diverge sharply in $κ_T$: the former relies on an expanding standing store of unreused mappings, whereas the latter reuses compact internal structure. The note therefore reframes classic disputes about understanding by separating outward performance from the organisation that sustains it, and motivates why this distinction may matter for later AI-safety analysis.

</details>


### [117] [AutoFOAM: The Self-Refining Autonomous OpenFOAM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00003)
*Arun Govind Neelan,A Seshaditya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoFOAM是一个自我进化的LLM智能体，基于Qwen-coder 2.5-14B微调，能通过自然语言指令自动创建、评估、运行和进化OpenFOAM仿真，通过7阶段进化循环和三种抗崩溃策略加速CFD工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源CFD求解器（如OpenFOAM）使用门槛高，需要大量专业知识和耗时的配置文件设置，本文旨在通过LLM智能体降低这一负担。

Method: 基于Qwen-coder 2.5-14B预训练模型，在252个文本提示上微调（覆盖7个OpenFOAM求解器、13个参数化网格模板和y plus感知数值策略）；核心是7阶段进化循环算法，并采用三种互补的抗崩溃流：RAG增强重试上下文、词典级修补、提示多样性改写。

Result: AutoFOAM能够根据自然语言指令自主完成OpenFOAM仿真的创建、评估、运行和进化，有效防止模型在重复自训练中退化，加速快速原型开发。

Conclusion: 通过将生成式AI与严格的流体仿真相结合，AutoFOAM加速了快速原型设计并民主化了高级CFD工作流，降低了CFD仿真的使用门槛。

Abstract: Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) plays an important role in modern engineering, but using open-source solvers such as OpenFOAM requires considerable knowledge and skills, as well as time-consuming configuration file setup. To reduce this burden, we propose AutoFOAM - a self-evolving large language model (LLM) agent that creates, evaluates, runs, and evolves its own OpenFOAM simulations based solely on natural-language instructions. Our model is pre-trained on the Qwen-coder 2.5-14B, which is then fine-tuned on 252 text prompts targeting 7 OpenFOAM solvers, 13 parametrized mesh templates, and a y plus-aware numerical policy. The crucial element of the algorithm is a sophisticated evolution loop composed of 7 stages. To prevent model degeneration under repeated self-training, the agent employs three complementary anti-collapse streams: RAG-augmented retry context, surgical dictionary-level patching, and prompt-diversity paraphrasing. By bridging generative artificial intelligence with rigorous fluid simulations, AutoFOAM accelerates rapid prototyping and democratizes advanced CFD workflows.

</details>


### [118] [Enhancing LLMs with Context-Specific Knowledge for Mitigating Misinformation in SMEs: A RAG-based Modeling and Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00006)
*Md. Samiul Islam,Iqbal H. Sarker,Chadni Islam,Ahmad Mohsin,Ahmed Ibrahim,Helge Janicke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VectorRAG和GraphRAG两种检索增强生成方法，用于缓解中小企业在使用大语言模型时出现的幻觉和错误信息问题，并在LLaMA、Mistral、Qwen等多个LLM上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中小企业越来越多地采用大语言模型（LLM）来支持问答和业务决策，但LLM生成的幻觉（hallucination）可能成为错误信息来源，降低用户对模型可靠性和可信度的信心。亟需方法来解决这一问题。

Method: 提出了两种检索增强生成（RAG）建模方法：VectorRAG（基于向量检索）和GraphRAG（基于图结构检索），将外部知识源整合到LLM建模过程中以缓解幻觉。在多种主流LLM（LLaMA、Mistral、Qwen）上进行了实验评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，RAG增强的LLM能够显著提升响应质量，减少幻觉和错误信息，从而在中小企业环境中支持更可靠、可信且具有上下文感知能力的决策。

Conclusion: RAG增强方法（VectorRAG和GraphRAG）可以有效缓解LLM幻觉问题，提升中小企业决策支持系统的可靠性和可信度，为实际应用提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs), a part of artificial intelligence (AI), are increasingly being adopted by Small and Medium Enterprises (SMEs) to enhance question-answering capabilities and support business decision-making processes. However, hallucinations in LLM-generated outputs can serve as a source of misinformation, reducing user confidence in their reliability and trustworthiness within SMEs. Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has emerged as a promising approach to address this challenge by incorporating external knowledge sources into the modeling process. In this paper, we present VectorRAG and GraphRAG modeling approaches to mitigate hallucinations and misinformation risks and evaluate their effectiveness in SME environments. Our experimental evaluation is conducted on multiple state-of-the-art LLMs, including LLaMA, Mistral, and Qwen, to assess performance in terms of useful response generation, risk of hallucination, contextual relevance, as well as human-interpretation. The results demonstrate that RAG-enhanced LLMs can significantly improve response quality by reducing hallucinations and misinformation, thereby supporting more reliable, trustworthy, and context-aware decision-making in SME environments.

</details>


### [119] [Energy Efficiency of Locally Deployed LLMs: A Preliminary Quantitative GPU Power Benchmark on Consumer Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00008)
*Philipp M. Zähl,Anika Hennig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可复现的硬件级能耗基准测试方法，在单张消费级GPU（RTX 4060Ti 16GB）上对9个开源LLM（1B到7B参数）进行能耗评估，发现模型架构和量化策略比参数量更能影响能效，其中gemma3:1b和llama3.2:1b能耗最低、吞吐最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的本地部署正因隐私需求和本地推理而日益流行，但消费级硬件上的能耗成本缺乏充分表征——现有基准只关注准确性而忽略能耗维度。

Method: 使用Ollama推理引擎在RTX 4060Ti 16GB GPU上运行9个开源LLM（1B-7B），通过nvidia-smi以2Hz频率采样GPU功耗，评估平均/峰值功率、每个提示的总能耗（J/prompt）、每个输出token的能耗（J/token）和吞吐量（tok/s）。

Result: gemma3:1b和llama3.2:1b能耗最低（分别0.56 J/token和0.65 J/token）且吞吐最高（>170 tok/s）；7B-Mistral每token能耗是最优模型的4.4倍；qwen3.5:2b因扩展的内部推理导致每个提示能耗异常偏高。

Conclusion: 影响LLM能效的因素超越原始参数量，模型架构和量化策略起着关键作用；能耗指标需要区分token生成模式（如推理与输出阶段）以获得准确的能效评估。

Abstract: The local deployment of large language models (LLMs) is gaining traction due to privacy concerns and the desire for on-premise inference. However, the energy costs on consumer hardware remain poorly characterized, as most benchmarks focus solely on accuracy. This paper presents a reproducible, hardware-level energy benchmark of nine open-source LLMs (1B to 7B parameters) executed on a single consumer GPU (RTX 4060Ti 16GB). Using the Ollama inference engine, GPU power draw was sampled at 2Hz via nvidia-smi across a fixed prompt set. We evaluate mean/peak power, total energy per prompt (J/prompt), energy per output token (J/token), and throughput (tok/s). Our findings suggest that factors beyond raw parameter count, including model architecture and quantization strategy, drive energy efficiency. Specifically, gemma3:1b and llama3.2:1b achieve the lowest energy cost (0.56 J/token and 0.65 J/token) and the highest throughput (>170 tok/s). In contrast, the 7B-Mistral model consumes up to 4.4x more energy per token than the most efficient model. Notably, qwen3.5:2b exhibits anomalously high per-prompt energy due to extended internal reasoning, highlighting the need to distinguish between token generation modes in efficiency metrics.

</details>


### [120] [CoT-Core: Accelerating LLM Evaluation via CoT-Aware Coreset Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00014)
*Qihua Pan,Zhenheng Tang,Peijie Dong,Xiang Liu,Huacan Wang,Bo Li,Xiaowen Chu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CoT-Core框架，利用零样本思维链推理轨迹在潜空间中对问题聚类，实现训练无关的核心问题选择，在降低LLM评估成本的同时保持高保真度评分估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评测在持续开发过程中计算开销巨大，现有核心集选择方法存在两个问题：基于项目反应理论的方法面临"冷启动"瓶颈（需要大量历史日志），而基于表面词法相似度的方法忽略了任务背后的推理流形。

Method: CoT-Core利用LLM生成零样本思维链（Chain-of-Thought）推理轨迹，将这些轨迹投影到潜空间中进行聚类，依据任务的内在逻辑等价性而非表面文本相似度来选择核心问题。该方法无需训练，不依赖历史日志。

Result: 在GSM8K、MMLU、MMLU-Pro和GPQA数据集上的实验表明，CoT-Core显著降低评测成本，同时保持高保真度的评分估计；此外还揭示了推理感知剪枝的边界条件，其有效性受任务复杂度的内在制约。

Conclusion: CoT-Core通过捕捉问题间的逻辑等价性实现高效且精准的核心问题选择，为LLM评测加速提供了新思路，并明确了该方法适用的任务复杂度边界。

Abstract: Evaluating Large Language Models (LLMs) incurs prohibitive computational overhead during continuous development processes. While coreset selection accelerates evaluation, existing methods either suffer from a severe ``cold start'' bottleneck requiring massive historical logs (e.g., Item Response Theory) or exhibit a surface lexical bias that misses the underlying reasoning manifold of tasks. We propose CoT-Core, a novel training-free core question selection framework. Recognizing that lexically disparate questions can share equivalent underlying logic, CoT-Core prompts LLMs to unroll zero-shot Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning trajectories. Projecting these paths into a latent space effectively clusters questions by intrinsic logical equivalence rather than superficial text similarity. Extensive experiments on GSM8K, MMLU, MMLU-Pro, and GPQA demonstrate that CoT-Core drastically reduces evaluation costs while maintaining high-fidelity score estimation, and delineate the boundary conditions of reasoning-aware pruning, revealing that its efficacy is intrinsically gated by task complexity.

</details>


### [121] [Optimization and Constraint Modeling using LLMs with a Retrieval Augmented Generation Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00015)
*Prateek Roy,Akash Singirikonda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于检索增强生成（RAG）的流水线，利用由Text2Zinc种子描述和LLM生成的专业角色构建的500个合成优化问题（编码于Chroma向量数据库），在Qwen 3 30B Instruct模型下将NL4OPT准确率从40%提升至72%、MAMO Easy从40%提升至56%、MAMO Complex从32%提升至56%，表明语义验证的合成示例结合检索增强是微调的有效替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化建模和约束建模需要深厚的领域专业知识，而现有大语言模型在组合优化场景中经常产生结构不一致或不完整的优化公式，因此需要探索一种无需昂贵模型重训练即可提升LLM优化建模能力的实用路径。

Method: 从Text2Zinc数据集提取种子描述，结合LLM生成的专业角色，合成500个以JSON格式规范化的优化问题，并关联经过验证的Python求解器脚本；将问题编码至Chroma向量数据库，推理时检索语义相似问题作为上下文指导，交由LangChain LLM代理生成优化公式。

Result: 在三组基准测试（NL4OPT、MAMO Easy、MAMO Complex）中，准确率分别从40%提升到72%、从40%提升到56%、从32%提升到56%，同时解决方案的准确性和结构完整性均得到显著改善。

Conclusion: 语义验证的合成示例搭配检索增强生成可大幅提升LLM优化建模的准确性和结构质量，为领域特定合成语料库配合RAG作为微调替代方案提供了有力证据，为实际决策支持场景中部署LLM优化工具开辟了低成本路径。

Abstract: Both optimization modeling and constraint modeling are non-trivial problems requiring deep domain expertise and proficiency in modeling formalism languages. Despite their importance across logistics, healthcare, and supply chain management, current large language models regularly produce structurally inconsistent or incomplete optimization formulations, particularly in combinatorial settings. This paper evaluates whether a Retrieval-Augmented Generation pipeline built on a curated synthetic dataset can meaningfully improve LLM optimization modeling performance. A total of 500 optimization problems were synthesized using seed descriptions from the Text2Zinc dataset and professional personas created using an LLM, specified in JSON and associated with validated Python solver scripts. These problems were encoded in a Chroma vector database. For each inference problem, semantically similar problems were retrieved and used as contextual guidance for a LangChain LLM agent. Three benchmark testbeds were used to evaluate the proposed pipeline under the Qwen 3 30B Instruct model. Accuracy rose from 40% to 72% on NL4OPT, 40% to 56% on MAMO Easy, and 32% to 56% on MAMO Complex. The use of semantically validated synthetic examples greatly improves both solution accuracy and structure. The combination of synthetic dataset generation with retrieval augmentation provides an effective alternative to fine-tuning, suggesting that domain-specific synthetic corpora paired with retrieval augmentation can serve as a practical pathway for deploying LLM-based optimization tools in real-world decision-support contexts without costly model retraining.

</details>


### [122] [Memory Reward Inflation in Self-Improving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00017)
*Mohammad Asadolahi,Amir Amini,Samira Talebi,Amirfarhad Farhadi,Azadeh Zamanifar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于外部记忆的LLM自我改进智能体中的奖励膨胀问题，提出"Echo Gap"失败模式，形式化了错误独立性假设（EIA）作为纠正奖励膨胀的必要条件，并提出了无答案的去膨胀算法LUCID，在BIRD文本转SQL基准上显著提升了执行准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆型LLM智能体在不更新权重的情况下通过存储、评分和检索经验来自我改进，但部署时缺乏真实标签，存储的奖励只能由LLM自身评估。这种替代会产生错误记忆被高估的问题，导致智能体反复使用自己最自信的错误，而现有方法无法识别哪些记忆被高估。

Method: 作者形式化了"Echo Gap"失败模式，提出错误独立性假设（EIA）并证明其是纠正奖励膨胀的必要条件——可用信号必须同时追踪真实性并且其误差与记忆偏差去相关。作者进一步证明膨胀不仅发生在按存储分数排序检索时，也发生在按相似度检索（实际部署场景）时。基于此提出了无答案的去膨胀算法LUCID。

Result: LUCID在BIRD文本转SQL基准上达到了56.9%的执行准确率，优于Memento风格的自评分智能体（54.0%，平均提升+2.9个百分点）和无记忆的相同架构智能体（52.4%）。

Conclusion: 记忆型自我改进智能体的奖励膨胀问题可以通过去相关化的信号设计来纠正，LUCID提供了有效的无答案去膨胀方案，且该问题在多种智能体和模型家族中普遍存在。

Abstract: Self-improving LLM agents increasingly learn from experience without updating any weights. Each episode is stored in an external memory, scored, and retrieved for similar future tasks to shape later behavior. Viewed through a reward lens, the stored score is a proxy reward for an implicit, non-parametric policy. Each retrieved episode then becomes a policy-improvement step whose reliability hinges on how that score is produced. In deployment, ground-truth labels are unavailable, so the stored reward is at best an LLM assessment. This substitution creates a failure mode, the *Echo Gap*, across the memory-based self-improving agents and model families studied. Incorrect episodes receive inflated rewards; thus, the agent preferentially reuses the very mistakes it has most confident in. Because the error compounds through memory rather than averaging out and the confirming judge's errors remain correlated with the original self-grading bias, so it cannot identify which memories are overvalued. The missing property is formalized as the *Error-Independence Assumption* (EIA), which we prove is a *necessary* condition for correcting the inflation, not merely a description of a good verifier: a usable signal must track truth *and* decorrelate its error from the memory bias, and the recoverable payoff is a closed-form function of exactly those two quantities. We further show the inflation compounds not only when retrieval ranks by the stored score but also under plain similarity retrieval which is the regime the deployed agent uses. Finally, the answer-free de-inflation algorithm LUCID delivers a consistent end-to-end gain on the BIRD text-to-SQL benchmark. It raises execution accuracy to $56.9\%$, above both a Memento-style self-graded agent ($54.0\%$, a $+2.9$-point mean gain across seeds) and a memory-less agent of identical architecture ($52.4\%$).

</details>


### [123] [Request-Level Energy Attribution for Batched LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00026)
*Qi Luo,Kunlin Li,Ziwen Wang,Dongsheng Wang,Yun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出JouleShare框架，通过离线精确计算Shapley能量归因建立请求级能耗真值，并用轻量校准模型JCalib在线预测公平的能耗分摊。实验表明按token比例的归因与精确Shapley存在显著偏差（归一化L1约0.44），而JCalib可将误差降至0.116-0.177，实现更公平的请求级能耗归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批处理LLM服务虽然提升吞吐量，但GPU能耗遥测是聚合的，无法提供请求级能耗。现有基准只报告模型级、阶段级或token级能耗，缺乏测量得到的请求级真值，导致实践中使用的能耗分摊规则与公平分配之间的偏差程度未知。

Method: JouleShare包含两个组件：（1）离线框架在vLLM下重放请求子集，集成GPU功耗遥测，计算每请求的精确Shapley能耗作为真值；（2）轻量校准模型JCalib从廉价请求特征学习预测Shapley分摊份额，用于在线服务时使用。还使用采样Shapley将测量参考扩展到更大请求组。

Result: 在16组模型/工作负载实验下：token比例归因与精确Shapley的平均归一化L1误差在静态批处理为0.440、连续批处理为0.458，且跨三种数据中心GPU一致复现。JCalib将误差降至静态0.116、连续0.177，低于不可在线获取的独立测量基线，同时保持精确批能量效率。

Conclusion: token归因不能可靠代理批量执行下的边际能耗；通过实测Shapley真值校准低成本请求特征可以实现更公平的能耗归因，为可持续性报告、成本分摊和工作负载分析提供更准确的请求级能耗依据。

Abstract: Batched LLM serving improves throughput but complicates energy accounting. GPU power telemetry is aggregate, whereas sustainability reporting, chargeback, and workload analysis often require request-level energy charges. Existing inference-energy benchmarks report model-, phase-, or token-level energy, and recent carbon-accounting work motivates Shapley fairness conceptually. Neither provides measured request-level ground truth, so how far the accounting rules used in practice deviate from a fair allocation has remained unknown. We present JouleShare, an attribution framework with two components. An offline harness establishes this ground truth by replaying request subsets under vLLM with a reproducible protocol, integrating GPU power telemetry, and computing exact Shapley energy for each request. A lightweight calibration model, JCalib, then learns to predict Shapley shares from cheap request features for use at serving time. Across 16 model/workload runs, token-proportional attribution differs from exact Shapley by 0.440 normalized L1 on average under static batching and by 0.458 under continuous batching, a gap that reproduces across three data-center GPUs. JCalib reduces this error to 0.116 under static batching and 0.177 under continuous batching, below even a standalone-measurement baseline that is unavailable online, while preserving exact batch-energy efficiency. Sampled Shapley extends the measured reference to larger group sizes, where the gap persists and a single offline calibration remains the most accurate deployable rule. The results show that token attribution is not a reliable proxy for marginal energy under batched execution, and that measured Shapley ground truth can calibrate low-cost request features toward fairer attribution.

</details>


### [124] [Motif-Mamba: network motif improved mamba for long-range sequence modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00027)
*Chonghe Hao,Yue Sun,Jian Zhang,Yansong Wang,Wangzi Yao,Yunjie Yao,Tielin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Motif-Mamba是一种结构化状态空间模型，通过引入基于三节点网络模体启发的低秩循环通路，增强Mamba状态维度间的显式交互，在保持线性时间复杂度的同时提升长序列建模能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mamba虽然通过选择性状态空间递归实现了线性时间建模，但其主要以对角状态转移为主，限制了状态维度之间的显式交互，这阻碍了模型对长序列中复杂依赖关系的捕捉。

Method: Motif-Mamba在Mamba架构中引入一个受三节点网络模体动力学启发的低秩循环通路：先将隐藏状态投影到紧凑的动态子空间，在该子空间中施加模体引导的维度间交互，再将动力学结果映射回原始状态空间，从而增强跨维度通信并保持线性递归结构。

Result: 在长序列外推、语言建模基准和脑机接口解码等实验中，Motif-Mamba相比Mamba基线模型取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 模体引导的低秩动力学为长序列建模提供了一种有效的结构化先验，表明在不牺牲线性时间效率的前提下，可以通过增强状态维度的结构化交互来提升模型性能。

Abstract: Efficient long-sequence modeling remains a central challenge for large language models, as self-attention scales quadratically with sequence length. Mamba offers a linear-time alternative through selective state space recurrence, but its predominantly diagonal state transitions restrict explicit interactions among state dimensions. We propose Motif-Mamba, a structured state space model that augments Mamba with a motif-constrained low-rank recurrent pathway. Inspired by the dynamics of three-node network motifs, the proposed pathway projects hidden states into a compact dynamical subspace, imposes motif-guided interactions, and maps the resulting dynamics back to the original state space. This design enhances cross-dimensional communication while preserving the linear-time recurrent structure of Mamba. Experiments on long-sequence extrapolation, language modeling benchmarks, and brain--computer interface decoding show consistent improvements over Mamba backbones, suggesting that motif-guided low-rank dynamics provide an effective structural prior for long-range sequence modeling.

</details>


### [125] [Nova: An End-to-End MLIR Compiler for Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00029)
*Adwaid Suresh,Aparna A,Harshini V M,Jona Delcy C A,Killi Uma Maheswara Rao,Ram Charan Golla,Surendra Vendra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Nova是一个自动化端到端JIT编译器，通过捕获eager执行、统一前后向传播为单值语义方言，实现全图级优化，利用解析配置器零搜索时间推导最优执行调度，并在消费级GPU上相比PyTorch和XLA获得更高的吞吐和更低的内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型扩展性能严重依赖高层数学运算到物理硬件的映射效率，而现有eager执行模型缺乏全图可见性和对硬件/内存的细粒度控制，无法原生最大化硬件利用率。

Method: Nova设计了三个核心组件：(1) 捕获eager执行并将前向和反向传播统一为单一值语义方言，解锁激进的全图优化；(2) Analytic Configurator基于算术强度解析推导最优执行调度，将搜索时间降为零；(3) 基于结构哈希的运行时直接从计算结构合成细粒度内核，实现寄存器级别的调优。

Result: 在RTX 3060上，Nova在大多形状下匹配或略优于cuBLAS和XLA的TF32矩阵乘法，误差小于5e-4；模型级别相比PyTorch吞吐提升10.6%，相比XLA提升4.4%；内存占用较PyTorch减少29%，在12GB GPU上成功训练1.44亿参数模型（17,900 tokens/s），而PyTorch遭遇OOM。

Conclusion: 通过全图优化、解析驱动的调度推导和结构化内核合成，Nova实现了对硬件映射的绝对控制，在无需搜索的情况下显著提升深度学习模型的吞吐和内存效率，尤其在高内存压力的消费级GPU场景下具有明显优势。

Abstract: The performance of deep learning models at scale relies heavily on how effectively high-level mathematical operations are mapped to underlying physical hardware. While high-level tensor frameworks provide flexible abstractions for model design, their eager execution models inherently lack the whole-graph visibility and granular control over hardware and memory required to maximize physical hardware utilization natively. To bridge this gap, we designed Nova, an automated end-to-end JIT compiler whose defining purpose is to achieve absolute control over this hardware mapping: fusing operations across operation boundaries, optimizing complex memory hierarchies, and tuning execution down to the register level. By capturing eager executions and unifying forward and backward passes into a single value-semantic dialect, Nova unlocks aggressive whole-graph optimizations. It then utilizes an Analytic Configurator to deterministically derive optimal execution schedules based on arithmetic intensity, dropping search time to zero. Backed by a structural hashing runtime, Nova synthesizes fine-grained kernels directly from the computation's structure. In our evaluations on an RTX 3060, Nova matches or modestly exceeds cuBLAS and XLA on TF32 matmuls on most shapes, maintaining a stringent < 5e-4 relative error. At the model level, Nova achieves up to 10.6% greater throughput than PyTorch and 4.4% greater than XLA on a 42-million parameter model, without compromising on numerical fidelity. Crucially, by reducing the memory footprint by up to 29% relative to PyTorch, Nova successfully trains a 144-million parameter model at 17,900 tokens/s where PyTorch encounters Out-Of-Memory (OOM) failures on the same 12 GB consumer GPU.

</details>


### [126] [SIRIN: A Unified Toolkit for Detecting Contextual Hallucinations in Retrieval-Augmented and Memory-Grounded LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00033)
